[\[1a.1\]]@#/_/rgyud 'bum tsa bzhugs so//
[\[1b\]]
[\[1b.1\]]@##/_/de nas hUM zhes bya ba dran pas khro bo'i rgyal po gshegs par 'gyur ro/_/las 'grub pa ston pa dang mthong ba sbyin pa dang 'dod pa'i mchog thams cad rab tu sbyin par 
[\[1b.2\]]'gyur ro/_/'on te 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug dang khro bo'i rgyal po spyan drang bar 'dod na/_shing su ra bis me sbar la/_'bras yos dang /_pad+ma dang ut+pa la dang /_yungs 
[\[1b.3\]]kar dang /_tsan+dan dang /_zho dang sbrang rtsi rnams mar dang sbyar te brgya rtsa brgyad sbyin sreg bya'o/_/rig sngags 'chang bas khrus legs par bya zhing gos gtsang ma bgos te gtsang bar bya'o/_/
[\[1b.4\]]de nas 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug dang khro bo'i rgyal po'i sku mdun na bzhugs shing rig sngags 'chang ba dbugs 'byin par mdzad do/_/mchog gang 'dod cing gsol ba'i 
[\[1b.5\]]mchog de thams cad sbyin par mdzad do/_/phyag g.yas pa mgo bo'i spyi bor 'jog par mdzad do/_/gal te skal ba chung ba zhig ste mngon du zhal mi mthong na de'i tshe rmi lam du bstan te/_
[\[2a\]]
[\[2a.1\]]thams cad bka' stsol cing ji ltar 'dod pa'i mchog sbyin te/_gsang sngags grub pa chen po dang /_cho ga zhib mo grub pa dang /_dkyil 'khor dang phyag rgya dang /_rim par phye ba grub par sbyin no/_/dam tshig grub pa chen po rab tu ston 
[\[2a.2\]]to/_/dbang phyug thob par bya na/_'bras so ba stu ra sa dang /_zho dang sbrang rtsi rnams mar dang sbyar te sbyin sreg brgya rtsa brgyad byas na dbang phyug so sor thob par 'gyur ro/_/grong thob par bya na/_tan tu li ya kha dang zho dang sbrang rtsi rnams mar dang sbyar te lan brgya 
[\[2a.3\]]rtsa brgyad sbyin sreg byas na grong thob par 'gyur ro/_/yul gyi bdag por bya na 'bras sA lu dang ras bal sa bon rnams mar dang bsres te brgya rtsa brgyad sbyin sreg byas na yul thob par 'gyur ro/_/mi snang bar bya na/_ske tshe dang lan tshwa'i sbyin sreg bya'o/_/
[\[2a.4\]]nam mkha' la 'gro bar bya na/_sha ta pad tri ka'i me tog gis sbyin sreg bya'o/_/sangs rgyas kyi sku mthong bar bya na/_shi li dang tsan+dan gyi phye ma dang /_tu ruSh+ka rnams til dang sbyar te lan brgya rtsa brgyad sbyin sreg byas na de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku mang po 
[\[2a.5\]]mthong bar 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa de dag gis rig sngags 'chang ba legs so legs so zhes gsung zhing dbugs 'byin to/_/de bzhin gshegs pa de dag thams cad kyis mgo bo'i spyi bor phyag 'jog cing mchog grub pa chen po stsol bar mdzad do/_/
[\[2a.6\]]thams cad du mchod pa 'os pa chen por 'gyur ro/_/shi nas bde ba can gyi 'jig rten gyi khams su skal ba mnyam par skye bar 'gyur ro/_/tshe rabs dran par 'gyur ro/_/'jig rten kun gyi bla mar 'gyur ro/_/dgra thams cad gzhom par 'dod na 'bras ma 
[\[2b\]]
[\[2b.1\]]grugs pas sbyin sreg bya'o/_/gter bsgul na de nas don yod pa chen po'i spyan sngar brjod par bya ste/_khro bo'i rgyal po'i dngos grub chen po sgyu ma'i dngos grub bla na med pa/_bklags pas grub pa so sor bstan pa'i dngos grub bla na med pa/_grub pa rigs sngags 'chang gi rnam 
[\[2b.2\]]par 'phrul pa ston pa lhur len pa bde ba skyed pa la mkhas pa rnams kyi rigs sngags 'chang gi dngos grub kyi de kho na nyid/_de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi de kho na nyid mchog tu gsang ba/_byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi rdzu 'phrul gyi rnam par 'phrul pa/_ngogs su zhugs pa 
[\[2b.3\]]rnams kyi rab tu sgrub pa/_dad pa'i rjes su 'brang ba rnams rnam par sel ba/_don gyi stegs mchog tu grub pa/_mal cha dang stan med pa rnams kyi gos sems can thams cad kyi mchod par bya ba chen po/_thams cad du las thams cad legs par grub pa/_sdig pa dang sgrib pa 
[\[2b.4\]]thams cad 'phrog pa/_gdug pa thams cad 'joms pa/_rgyal po mnyes par byed pa/_rnam par thar pa dang dul ba'i grong khyer nyid de/_dge bsnyen rnams kyi mchog tu skyid pa/_dge bsnyen ma rnams kyi dga' ba 'phel bar byed pa/_rig pa'i rgyal po'i yon tan rmad du byung 
[\[2b.5\]]ba'i de kho na nyid sgrub pa grub pa rnams gtsang ma gtsang sbrar spyod pa/_gos gtsang ma bgos te/_bza' ba mchog shin tu gsang ba dang /_bag yod cing bden par smra ba/_dkon mchog gsum la mchog tu dad pa/_bla ma la gus pa/_rtag par rgyun du byams pa'i sems la 
[\[2b.6\]]mnyam par gzhag pa/_sems can thams cad la brtse bar gyur pa/_sbyin pa la mngon par dga' ba'i yid can/_'byung po thams cad la brtse ba'i sems dang byams pas bla na med pa'i sgrub pa 'di bya'o/_/bslab pa dang sdom pa blangs pa/_don yod pa chen po'i sgyu 
[\[2b.7\]]ma bla na med pa ni 'di'o/_/gi wang dang /_spr-i ka dang /_pu shel tse dang /_tsan+dan dang /_ldong ros dang /_shing kun dang /_spang spos dang /_na la da dang /_ru rta dang bcas pa dang /_de bzhin du 'bras dgang ste 'di rnams thams cad cha mnyam ste/_phye mar byas nas/_
[\[3a\]]
[\[3a.1\]]thams cad zhib mor btags la/_ga bur dang /_gla rtsis bsgos te/_snyigs ma 'di dag tsha ba'i til mar gyis bcos pa/_nye'u shing dang /_bil ba dang /_me tog brgya pa dang /_puSh+ka ra dang /_gan+d+ha pri yang ku dang /_rgya spos dang /_sa mu sta dang /_pra 
[\[3a.2\]]puN+Da rI ka dang /_shing mngar rnams chu bre bdun gang bar sbyin par bya ste drug cha lhag mar lus pa dang /_snyigs ma bre gang dang 'o ma'i snyigs ma bor te/_til mar srang gnyis la rtag tu btso bar bya'o/_/legs par grub pa'i gsang sngags bzlas brjod byas la gtor ma dang /_
[\[3a.3\]]dkyil 'khor du me tog dang /_dri bzang po'i bdug pa dang /_til mar btso ba'i cho ga bzhin du rab tu sbyar la gang las grub pa khyad par du bskyed cing babs pa'i til mar byas pa srung ba'i cho ga bya ste/_snod gtsang ma bkang nas slar yang gsang sngags lan nyi shu rtsa gcig tu bzlas 
[\[3a.4\]]brjod byas nas gsang ba'i gnas su gzhag par bya'o/_/de nas las thams cad byed du gzhug ste/_rig sngags 'chang khrus legs par byas pa/_dris bsgos pa'i til mar rtag tu lus la byugs pa 'dis/_lus de mi snang zhing dngos po med par ston par byed do/_/ngag thos 
[\[3a.5\]]la reg par mi byed do/_/gang gis lus khrus ma byas kyi bar de srid du bdag nyid mi snang ste lus mi mthong ngo /_/dpral bar thig le byas nas/_yan lag dang /_nying lag rnams la reg ste/_gsang sngags lan cig bzlas brjod byas na nam mkha' la 'gro bar 'gyur te/_
[\[3a.6\]]skad cig la dga' bar byed pa dang /_lha dang bcas pa'i rig sngags 'chang gi dbang po mkha' la spyod pa rnams kyis lhan cig tu til mar mgo la byugs pas mdog sngo ba dang /_skra cung zad li zhing 'khyil bar 'gyur ro/_/til mar grub pa'i rig sngags 'chang thams cad kyi 'khor 
[\[3a.7\]]los sgyur ba'i mnga' bdag tu 'gyur ro/_/reg pa tsam gyis tshe bskal pa khri thub par 'gyur ro/_/lus pad+ma dkar sham lta bur 'gyur ro/_/lus rdo rje ltar brtan pa dang /_gnas kyi sgo thams cad bye ste 'dug go/_/gang du 'dod par 'jug par byed do/_/dkyil 'khor sgrub 
[\[3b\]]
[\[3b.1\]]pa'i dus su rig sngags 'chang la thig le byas nas lag tu rdo rje dang nor bu byas na/_nam mkha' la 'phur bar 'gyur te/_mod la dkyil 'khor grub pa ston par byed do/_/til mar gyis rkang pa la byugs na/_nam mkha' la 'ong bar byed do/_/lus la byugs na mi snang bar 
[\[3b.2\]]ston par byed do/_/mtshal dang til mar lhan cig sbyar te/_yan lag dang /_nying lag la thig le byas na/_lus 'bar ba'i snying po mthong bar byed do/_/'di dag rig sngags 'chang gi rnam 'phrul so sor bstan pa'o/_/yungs kar la bzlas te/_lan cig bskus sam/_
[\[3b.3\]]dkyil 'khor gyi steng du gtor na/_dkyil 'khor g.yo bar 'gyur te/_'khor lo bzhin du bskor bar byed do/_/gtor ma'i snod gang ba rnams dang /_bum pa gang ba rnams sor mo brgyad kyi tshad du nam mkha' la 'phags te 'dug go/_/rang nyid se gol gyi sgras yongs su 'don 
[\[3b.4\]]par byed na/_slar yang snod nam mkha' la 'phags te 'dug pa rang rang gi gnas su gnas par 'gyur ro/_/'di rnams dkyil 'khor dgos pa bla na med pa'i dngos grub bo/_/de nas seng ge'i khrag dang til mar lhan cig sbyar te/_bdag nyid kyi lus la byugs na/_
[\[3b.5\]]mi yi seng ge chen po'i gzugs su mthong bar 'gyur te/_ji srid lus khrus ma byas pa de srid du mi'i seng ger kun tu gnas so/_/gzhan yang ji ltar bsams pa bzhin du/_nam mkha' la 'phags te 'dug pa dang /_mi snang bar 'gyur ba dang /_me 'bar ba ston par byed 
[\[3b.6\]]do/_/phag gi khrag dang sbyar na/_phag gi gzugs su ston par byed de/_shugs dang stobs chen por ston par byed do/_/glang po che'i khrag dang sbyar bas tshogs kyi dbang phyug chen po'i gzugs su ston par byed do/_/rta'i khrag dang sngon po lhan cig sbyar ba lus la byugs pa 
[\[3b.7\]]tsam gyis rta mgrin gyi gzugs su ston par byed de ji srid khrus ma byas pa de srid du mthong bar byed do/_/ba lang gi mkhris pa dang lhan cig sbyar na/_/khyu mchog chen po la zhon par mthong bar 'gyur ro/_/nya'i mkhris pa dang lhan cig sbyar na/_klu'i rgyal po chu bdag chen po'i gzugs 
[\[4a\]]
[\[4a.1\]]su mthong bar 'gyur ro/_/sbrul dmar po'i khrag dang bsres nas sbyar na/_sbrul chen po'i gzugs su mthong bar 'gyur ro/_/ra'i khrag dang bsres pas/_ra chen po'i gzugs su mthong bar 'gyur ro/_/pad+ma'i lo ma dang /_phye ma dang /_rtsa ba dang bcas pa 
[\[4a.2\]]dang /_'dab ma dang me tog dang sdong bu dang bcas pa til mar gyis lhan cig sbyar bas yungs kar la bskus te lus la byugs la til mar gyis thig le bya'o/_/mkhas pas yan lag dang nying lag la byug pa byas na rgyal po chen po'i cha lugs su mthong bar 'gyur te/_ma mo'i tshogs dang 
[\[4a.3\]]bcas pas yongs su bskor bas dgos pa sna tshogs pa dang /_rgyal po'i dgos pa rnams byed par 'gyur ro/_/yid la bsams pa'i dgos pa thams cad sgrub par byed par 'gyur ro/_/ldong ros kyi mig sman dang lhan cig bsres nas mig la byugs na/_'khor chen po'i dbus su/_
[\[4a.4\]]spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi gzugs su mthong bar 'gyur ro/_/rig pa'i rgyal po bzlas te yungs kar de nam mkha' la gtor na/_'bras bu sna tshogs kyi char 'bab par mthong bar 'gyur ro/_/pad+ma'i snying pos sbyar na/_pad+ma chen po'i char 'byung bar 'gyur ro/_/ut+pa 
[\[4a.5\]]la sngon po'i khu bas sbyor na/_ut+pa la sngon po chen po'i char 'byung ba mthong bar 'gyur ro/_/ji ltar me tog gi khu bas sbyar ba de lta de ltar me tog gi char chen po 'byung bar 'gyur ro/_/ji ltar yid la 'dod pa'i gzugs dang /_kha dog dang /_rtags dang /_dbyibs su mthong bar 
[\[4a.6\]]'dod pa de lta de ltar lha'i til mar rnying pa dang lhan cig sbyar na/_de lta de ltar de dang de nyid kyi gzugs su mthong bar 'gyur ro/_/gzhal yas khang yang mthong bar 'gyur ro/_/lag pas nor bu gcig blangs te/_yid la rjes su dran par byas na/_rnam pa sna tshogs pa du ma'i bdag nyid 
[\[4a.7\]]du mthong bar 'gyur ro/_/gos dang /_rgyan sna tshogs pa rnams ston par byed do/_/lag pas snod la gzung na/_snod sna tshogs pa bton par byed do/_/lag pas bum pa la gzung na/_bum pa brgya stong phrag ston par byed do/_/rkang pa'i mthil la bskus na/_gang du rdo ba la rkang pa 
[\[4b\]]
[\[4b.1\]]bzhag pa de dang de nyid du rkang pa'i rjes 'jog par byed de/_mar gsar la nye bar 'jog pa ji lta ba bzhin du/_mar gsar mnyen pa bzhin du rdo ba yang 'dra bar 'gyur ro/_/gtun shing la byugs na/_rtags 'byung bar 'gyur ro/_/ma mo'i dkyil 'khor gyi gnas su zhugs na/_
[\[4b.2\]]ma mo'i lha rnams kyi so so tha dad pa'i rdul bzung ste til mar dang lhan cig sbyar nas so so'i gdong la byugs na/_lha de dang de lta bu'i gzugs su ston par byed do/_/gzhan yang dgos pa rnam pa sna tshogs pa rnams rang gi 'dod pa'i blos las bya'o/_/de nas blo 'phel bar byed pa 
[\[4b.3\]]ni/_til mar dang /_'o ma lhan cig zos na/_blo dang dran pa chen po 'phel bar 'gyur ro/_/nub gsum gyi sbyor bas tshigs su bcad pa stong klog par byed cing 'dzin par byed do/_/zhag bdun gyi sbyor bas blo gros rgya mtsho chen po lta bu 'phel bar byed cing sogs par byed do/_/
[\[4b.4\]]zhag bcu bzhi'i sbyor bas tshe lha'i lo stong du 'gyur ro/_/zhag nyi shu rtsa gcig gi sbyor bas/_bcud len sgrub pa'i dngos grub 'byung bar 'gyur te/_gnyer ma dang /_skra dkar spangs pa dang /_skra cung zad 'khyil bar 'gyur ro/_/lus 'jam zhing mnyen pa dang /_pad+ma'i 
[\[4b.5\]]kha dog dang 'dra bar 'gyur ro/_/skyes nas lo bcu lon pa dang 'dra bar gzugs dang lang tsho dang ldan par 'gyur ro/_/glang po che stong phrag bcu'i stobs su 'gyur ro/_/zhag nyi shu rtsa brgyad kyi sbyor bas glang po ches srung gi stobs su 'gyur ro/_/zhag sum cu so lnga'i sbyor bas 
[\[4b.6\]]nam mkha' lding chen po'i stobs su 'gyur ro/_/zhag bzhi bcu rtsa gnyis kyi sbyor bas lha ma yin gyi dbang po sgra gcan dang 'dra bar brtson 'grus dang stobs chen por 'gyur ro/_/zhag bzhi bcu rtsa dgu'i sbyor bas ri rab lhun po chen po'i dkyil 'khor dang 'dra bar 'gyur te/_klu'i 
[\[4b.7\]]rgyal po dga' bo dang /_nye dga' bos yongs su bskor ba/_mi g.yo zhing mi bsgul ba/_reg par bya ba ma yin zhing mi mthong ba/_mi ma yin pa rnams kun tu spyod pa/_lha rnams thams cad kyis phyag byas shing /_phyag bya ba'i 'os su gyur pa/_drang srong rnams kyis bstod pa'i

[\[5a.1\]]'os su gyur pa/_gnod sbyin dang srin po rnams kyi phyag bya ba'i 'os su gyur pa/_de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis byin gyis brlabs pa/_byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi rab tu bsngags pa'i 'os su gyur pa/_rig sngags 
[\[5a.2\]]'chang ba'i dbang po thams cad kyi gsang ba'i dam tshig grub pa/_lha'i dbang po rnams kyis lhan cig 'gran pa'i 'os su gyur pa/_tshangs pa dang /_khyab 'jug dang /_dbang phyug chen po rnams kyi mngon du smra ba'i 'os su gyur pa/_klu'i dbang po thams cad kyi nye bar bgrod par bya ba 
[\[5a.3\]]ste/_de ltar don yod pa'i lcags kyu'i khro bo'i rnam par 'phrul pa zhes bya ba til mar gyi rgyal po'i rnam par 'phrul pa yon tan chen po rmad du byung ba brgya stong phrag gis gang ba'o/_/til mar gyi rgyal po'i rig sngags 'chang 'dis/_til mar gyi rgyal po ral gri la byugs na/_byugs 
[\[5a.4\]]pa tsam gyis ral gri 'bar bar 'gyur ro/_/rig sngags 'chang gi 'khor los sgyur bar 'gyur te/_ral gri'i rig sngags 'chang gi 'khor los sgyur ba thams cad kyi mchog tu 'gyur ro/_/rdo rje la byugs na rdo rje 'bar bar 'gyur te/_rig sngags 'chang gi 'khor los sgyur ba nam mkha' la 'gro 
[\[5a.5\]]zhing /_rdo rje'i rig sngags 'chang gi 'khor los sgyur bar 'gyur ro/_/'khor lo la byugs na 'khor lo 'bar bar 'gyur te/_rig sngags 'chang nam mkha' la 'gro bar 'gyur ro/_/blta na sdug pa'i 'khor lo'i rig sngags 'chang gi 'khor los sgyur bar mod la 'gyur ro/_/
[\[5a.6\]]dgra thams cad rab tu 'joms so/_/mdung rtse gsum pa la byugs na 'bar bar 'gyur te/_rig sngags 'chang gi 'khor los sgyur bar 'gyur te/_nam mkha' la gnas par byed do/_/stobs dang /_brtson 'grus dang /_pha rol gnon pa dbang phyug chen po dang 'dra bar 'gyur ro/_/
[\[5a.7\]]'bras dang /_phol mig dang /_lhog pa dang /_me dbal dang /_shu ba dang /_mdze dang /_rkang shu dang /_rtsa'i rma dang /_mgul pa 'gags pa dang /_rna ba'i rtsa nad dang /_mtshan bar rdol ba dang /_dmu chu dang /_skran dang /_rlung dang /_mkhris pa dang /_bad kan dang /_'dus pa

[\[5b.3\]]rnams rgyas pa dang /_mgu ba dang /_spro ba chen por 'gyur ro/_/gdon rnam pa sna tshogs par bsrung ba dang /_brjed byed mi bzad pa la yang /_reg pa tsam gyis de dag thams cad rnam par 'jig par the tshom med do/_/byad mi bzad pa dang /_sdug bsngal dang /_dug dang /_
[\[5b.4\]]sbyar dug nyid kyang reg pa tsam gyis rnam par 'jig par 'gyur ro/_/brtan pa dang /_g.yo ba'i dug gang yin pa dang /_mche ba'am/_sbrul go Na sa dang /_sdig pa yang rung /_srin bu dang /_phye ma leb gdug pa'am/_nor rgyas kyi bu dang /_'jog po'am/_sbrul gyis mthong ba'i 
[\[5b.5\]]dug gam/_dbugs kyi dug mi bzad pa gang yin pa dang /_reg pa'i dug gang yin pa dang /_hA la hA la'i gdug pa gang yin pa dang /_gzhan da ra da'i dug gang yin pa dang /_rtsa ba'i dug gang yin pa dang /_sdong bu'i dug dang /_'dab ma'i dug dang /_me tog gi dug dang /_mdangs 
[\[5b.6\]]kyi dug gang yin pa dang /_gzhan dug rnam pa sna tshogs pa de rnams thams cad rnam par 'jig par 'gyur ro/_/til mar reg pa tsam gyis/_lam mchog dang /_lam gol bar song ba rnams dang /_rgyal po'i sgo drung du song ba yang rung /_bsad par 'os par gyur pa gang yin pa dang /_
[\[5b.7\]]bcings par gyur pa gang yin pa dang /_'thab pa dang /_rtsod pa dang /_'gyed pa gang yin pa dang /_gzhan yang dgra'i gzeb dang /_pha rol gyi dmag tshogs kyi 'jigs pa nyid dang /_mi mdza' ba dang /_phyir rgol ba gang yin pa yang til mar 'di mngon par sbyar bas skad cig de la thams 
[\[6a\]]
[\[6a.1\]]cad 'joms so/_/rgyal po khros pa gang yin pa 'dzum bzhin du bka' stsol to/_/g.yul 'gyed pa'i nang du'am/_phan tshun 'thab bzhin pa thams cad la til mar rgyal po bla na med pas rtag tu rgyal ba ster ro/_/mkhas pas mig la byugs na/_mi thub cing thams cad 
[\[6a.2\]]du 'gro bar 'gyur ro/_/gzhan gyis kyang dgos pa thams cad la rab tu sbyar bar bya ste/_dngos grub kyi gnas thams cad kyi mchog yin pa'i phyir ro/_/de nas khro bo lcags kyu bla na med pa/_sdig pa thams cad sel ba/_mod la gdug pa thams cad 'jigs par byed pa/_nad thams cad 
[\[6a.3\]]rab tu zhi bar byed pa/_mod la thams cad du grub pa bla na med pa/_nad thams cad zhi ba/_de kho na nyid khro bo lcags kyu bla na med pa'i ras ris kyi sku gzugs brjod par bya ste/_ras ris kyi sku gzugs bla na med pa 'di mthong ba tsam gyi mod la/_bsod nams kyi phung po chen po thob pa dang /_
[\[6a.4\]]dge ba'i rtsa ba chen po bskyed par 'gyur ba dang /_rig pa'i gnas thams cad rtogs par khong du chud par 'gyur ba dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas gang+gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phrag dgu bcu rtsa gnyis mthong ba dang /_
[\[6a.5\]]mchod pa dang /_rim gror bya ba dang /_zhe sar bya ba dang /_bla mar byas pa dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa de rnams kyi spyan sngar bsod nams kyi phung po chen po'i dge ba'i rtsa ba dang ldan par 'gyur ba dang /_ras ris kyi sku gzugs gang yin pa 'di mthong ba tsam gyi mod la/_mthas klas 
[\[6a.6\]]pa tsam dang 'di mtshungs par 'gyur te/_'di dang mtshungs pa'i bsod nams kyi phung po'i dge ba'i rtsa ba bskyed par 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa' thams cad dang /_lha thams cad kyis dgongs par 'gyur ro/_/ras gru bzhi pa gru bzhir cha 
[\[6a.7\]]mnyam pa/_shin tu thags bzang ba/_shin tu gsar ba/_shin tu 'jam pa mchog tu mdzes pa/_khru gnyis pa'i tshad kha tshar ma bcad pa/_skra dang bral ba'am/_yang na dar gtsang ma gru bzhi shin tu rnam par 'byes pa la/_ri mo mkhan gtsang ma smyung bar gnas pas/_lhag

[\[15a.5\]]bzhin 'dzum pas/_bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das/_rdzu 'phrul gyi rnam par 'phrul pa che zhing che ba yang dag par snang ba dang /_mchod pa'i sprin gyi char che zhing che ba rab tu 'bab pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das dngos grub kyi mtshan ma che 
[\[15a.6\]]zhing che ba snang ba dang /_lha dang /_klu dang /_gnod sbyin dang /_dri za dang /_lha ma yin dang /_nam mkha' lding dang /_mi'am ci dang /_lto 'phye chen po dang /_mi dang mi ma yin pa'i 'khor chen po 'dus par gyur pa'i rig sngags kyi rgyal po skyes par mthong bar snang ba dang /_cho 
[\[15a.7\]]'phrul che zhing che ba snang ba dang /_rdzu 'phrul gyi rnam par 'phrul ba mthong ba dang /_byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi rig sngags chen po 'byung bar snang ba ni/_bcom ldan 'das rgyu ci lags/_rkyen ci lags/_'di ci'i 
[\[15b\]]
[\[15b.1\]]mthus sngon gyi mtshan ma yang dag par snang ba lags/_'di rdzu 'phrul gyi rnam par 'phrul pa gang zhig yang dag par snang ba lags/_bcom ldan 'das bdag gi sems la the tsom de skyes par gyur pas/_de nges par bstan du gsol/_zhu ba lung ston par 'gyur ba gang gis/_bdag gi 
[\[15b.2\]]dogs pa rnam par sel ba dang /_som nyi rnam par sel bar byed pas bcom ldan 'das kyis bdag la skabs dbye ru gsol/_de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa shAkya thub pas gnod sbyin gyi sde dpon chen po lag na rdo rje la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/lag na rdo rje nges 
[\[15b.3\]]par bstan par bya'i nyon cig_/lag na rdo rje 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi sgyu ma lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin gyi rnam par 'phrul ba'o/_/sgyu ma lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_rdo rje lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_dpa' bar 'gro 
[\[15b.4\]]ba'i ting nge 'dzin la sogs pa ting nge 'dzin gang+gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phrag dgu bcu rtsa dgus rnam par 'phrul pa'o/_/lag na rdo rje spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi mthus rdzu 'phrul gyi cho 'phrul snang bar byed do/_/don yod pa rnam par 'phrul pa/_kun tu snang ba'i dkyil 
[\[15b.5\]]'khor chen po dang /_phyag rgya dang /_cho ga zhib mo dang /_gsang sngags dang /_gzungs dang /_rig sngags kyi rgyal po'i rnam par 'phrul pa dang /_rig sngags kun tu snang ba dang /_rig sngags kyi rgyal po rnam par snang bar byed do/_/de'i mthus snang ba 'di dang /_rdzu 'phrul gyi rnam 
[\[15b.6\]]par 'phrul pa dang /_cho 'phrul 'di snang ngo /_/lag na rdo rje rig sngags kyi rgyal po 'di mthong ba dang /_ming bzung ba tsam gyis kyang /_de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas gang+gA'i klung gi bye ma bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phrag dgu bcu rtsa gnyis kyi rdul phra 
[\[15b.7\]]rab dang mnyam pa mngon sum du mthong ba dang /_mtshan gzung ba'i dge ba'i rtsa ba bskyed pa'i bsod nams kyi phung po chen po dang ldan pa yongs su 'dzin par 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rigs kyi dam tshig dang /_rig sngags dang /_phyag rgya dang /_gsang sngags 
[\[16a\]]
[\[16a.1\]]rim par phye ba dang /_snying po'i mthu rab tu 'jug par 'gyur ro/_/dam tshig gi dkyil 'khor gsang bar rab tu zhugs par 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi cho ga zhib mo'i rjes su 'jug par 'gyur ro/_/rig sngags kyi rgyal po 'di lan cig bzlas 
[\[16a.2\]]pa de rnams kyang /_de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi drung du/_'das pa dang /_ma 'ongs pa dang /_da ltar byung ba'i dus gsum du gshegs pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi snying po dang /_phyag rgya dang /_gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi dam tshig dang /_gsang sngags

[\[16a.5\]]no/_/snying po gcig po 'di/_rigs thams cad kyi dkyil 'khor dang /_phyag rgya rim par phye ba dang /_cho ga zhib mo'i rjes su zhugs pa'o/_/des na lag na rdo rje khyod kyis rig sngags kyi rgyal po 'di/_kun nas rjes su 'jug pa zhes bya'o/_/lag na rdo rje rgyu des na/_rigs 
[\[16a.6\]]kyi bu'am/_rigs kyi bu mo'am/_dge slong ngam/_dge slong ma'am/_dge bsnyen nam/_dge bsnyen ma rig sngags kyi rgyal po 'di/_ming lan cig gzung ba tsam gyis dus gsum du gshegs pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das thams cad kyi drung du/_gsang ba thams

[\[16b\]]
[\[16b.1\]]yongs su gzung bar 'gyur ro/_/dge ba'i rtsa ba chen po dang ldan par 'gyur ro/_/dus gsum du gshegs pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de rnams dang /_byang chub sems dpa' legs so zhes bya ba stsol bar 'gyur ro/_/don yod pa yangs pa chen po'i dngos grub

[\[17b.1\]]do/_/dug dang /_sbyar dug dang /_phye ma'i sbyor ba rnams thams cad 'byer bar byed do/_/rims rnams thams cad 'byor bar 'gyur ro/_/nyin gcig pa dang /_nyin gnyis pa dang /_nyin gsum pa dang /_nyin bzhi pa dang /_mtshan gcig pa dang /_nyin gcig pa dang /_grang ba'i rims

[\[18a\]]
[\[18a.1\]]sngags kyi rgyal po/_dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad dang /_bsod nams kyi tshogs thams cad dang /_gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi dam tshig gi dngos grub bla na med pa thams cad yang dag par rdzogs par byed pa'i rig sngags kyi rgyal po mchog tu dkon te/_de bzhin gshegs pa dang

[\[18a.3\]]pa de dag thams cad la phan pa'i don dang /_dngos grub mthong ba'i de kho na nyid kyi sgrub pa grub pa ched du 'gyur ro/_/dge ba'i rtsa ba mchog tu nye bar bsags par 'gyur ba las gzhan ma yin no/_/rig sngags kyi rgyal po 'di dran pa tsam gyis bsod nams kyi phung po dge ba'i rtsa ba'i tshogs

[\[18a.6\]]pa shAkya thub pas bka' stsal pa/_lag na rdo rje de de bzhin no/_/ji ltar khyod kyis smras pa de de bzhin te/_rig sngags kyi rgyal po 'di lag tu son par 'gyur ba'i sems can de ni dge ba'i rtsa ba chung ngu skyed pa ma yin no/_/lag na rdo rje gang+gA'i klung gi bye ma bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phrag dgu bcu 
[\[18a.7\]]rtsa dgu'i dus gsum mnyam pa nyid kyi de bzhin gshegs pa rnams ma mthong ba dang /_bsnyen bkur ma byas pa dang /_phyag rgya dang /_dkyil 'khor dang /_dam tshig dang /_gsang sngags rim par phye ba dang /_rig sngags kyi cho ga zhib mo'i rgyal po ma thos shing ma zhugs pa gang yin pa des ni/_
[\[18b\]]
[\[18b.1\]]lag na rdo rje rig sngags kyi rgyal po 'di de'i lag tu phyin par mi 'gyur ro/_/mi thos shing mi mthong la/_ming gi sgra grags pa tsam du'ang mi 'gyur ro/_/lag na rdo rje rig sngags kyi rgyal po 'di/_gang gi lag tu son par 'gyur ba des ni/_lag na rdo rje gang+gA'i klung gi bye ma bye ba khrag khrig brgya 
[\[18b.2\]]stong phrag dgu bcu rtsa dgu'i dus gsum mnyam pa nyid kyi de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams de'i lag tu son par 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa de rnams kyi drung du/_rigs kyi dam tshig gi dkyil 'khor dang /_phyag rgya dang /_gsang 
[\[18b.3\]]sngags rim par phye ba dang /_snying po dang /_gsang ba'i cho ga zhib mo thams cad lag tu son par 'gyur ro/_/gnas thams cad du song zhing rjes su zhugs par 'gyur ro/_/thams cad rjes su song zhing /_dam tshig shes par 'gyur ro/_/rig sngags thams cad dang /_gsang sngags rim 
[\[18b.4\]]par phye ba dang /_phyag rgya yang dag par spyod par 'gyur ro/_/rig sngags 'chang thams cad kyi mchog dang /_gtso bo dang thu bor 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa de rnams thams cad kyi drung na/_sras kyi gtso bor dus gsum du gshegs pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyis lung ston par 
[\[18b.5\]]'gyur ro/_/dge ba'i rtsa ba nye bar ston pa dang /_bsod nams kyi tshogs yongs su 'dzin par 'gyur ro/_/lag na rdo rje rig sngags kyi rgyal po 'di/_gang gi lag tu son cing thob par 'gyur ba'i sems can gang yin pa de ni/_de'i yon tan dang /_phan yon gzhal du med pa thob par 'gyur ro/_/
[\[18b.6\]]rig sngags kyi rgyal po 'di/_gang gi lag tu son par 'gyur ba de/_sangs rgyas kyi phun sum tshogs pa thams cad so sor rnyed par 'gyur ro/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gis/_rig sngags kyi rgyal po 'di smras nas/_rang 
[\[18b.7\]]gi gzugs kyi bdag nyid kyi dngos po pad+ma'i ze 'bru'i dbus su rig sngags kyi rgyal po 'phags par bltas nas/_don yod pa yid bzhin nor bu'i 'od ces bya ba nor bu rin po che chen po rig sngags kyi rgyal po bcom ldan 'das la phul bar gyur to/_/oM a mo g+ha tsin+tA ma Ni bar da pad+me dz+wa la 
[\[19a\]]
[\[19a.1\]]dz+wa la na b+hu dze hU~M/_'di ni don yod pa pad+ma dri ma med pa'i chu skyes kyi zhags pa'i rtsa ba'i snying po'o/_/'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gis/_bcom ldan 'das la phul ba'i de ma thag pa de nas rig sngags kyi rgyal po thams cad/_bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan snga na/_
[\[19a.2\]]bcom ldan 'das la spyan mi 'dzums par blta zhing 'khod do/_/bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pas don yod pa yid bzhin nor bu'i 'od rig sngags kyi rgyal po nor bu rin po che chen po de la yang /_rig sngags kyi rgyal pos spyi bo nas dbang bskur ro/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis rig sngags 
[\[19a.3\]]kyi rgyal po 'dis dbang bskur ma thag tu/_stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams ri dang /_nags dang 'brog dgon par bcas pa rnams rnam par 'gyur ba drug tu g.yos so/_/phyogs bcu kun nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad mthong bar gyur to/_/des na dus gsum 
[\[19a.4\]]du bzhugs pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas thams cad nam mkha'i dbyings su rdzu 'phrul gyis bzhugs te/_bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa shAkya thub pa la legs so zhes bya ba byin te/_shAkya thub pa mchog tu rnyed par dka' 
[\[19a.5\]]ba'i mngon par dbang bskur ba'i rgyal po 'dis mngon par dbang bskur ba 'di legs so legs so/_/dus gsum du gshegs pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa snga ma rnams kyis kyang de bzhin du rig pa'i rgyal po 'dis dbang bskur ro/_/nged thams cad kyis kyang tshig gcig tu mthun pas don 
[\[19a.6\]]yod pa yid bzhin nor bu'i 'od la rig sngags kyi rgyal po chen pos mngon par dbang bskur ro/_/dus gsum du gshegs pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa de rnams kyang phyag gser gyi kha dog can brkyang nas/_de rnams thams cad la rig pa'i rgyal pos spyi bo'i gtsug nas mngon par dbang bskur ro/_/
[\[19a.7\]]'di ni mngon par dbang bskur ba'i phyag rgya mngon par dbang bskur ba dang ldan pa'o/_/oM a mo g+ha bai ro tsa na ma hA mu drA ma Ni pad+me dz+wa la pra bar+d+ha ya hU~M/_phyag rgya chen pos mngon par dbang bskur ma thag tu/_bcom ldan 'das la legs so zhes bya ba byin te/_rang rang gi sangs rgyas kyi zhing du gshegs nas 
[\[19b\]]
[\[19b.1\]]mi snang bar gyur to/_/de nas rig pa'i rgyal po de de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis phyag rgyas dbang bskur ba rig nas/_bcom ldan 'das la g.yas su bskor ba gsum byas nas thal mo sbyar ba btud de/_phyag byas nas spyan sngar 'dug ste sems par gyur to/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das rig 
[\[19b.2\]]sngags 'chang de'i rig pa'i rgyal po sems par gzigs nas/_ji ltar da ltar yid la byed pa yang dag par rab tu gsal bar mdzad do/_/oM a mo g+ha maN+Da la pad+ma a b+hi She kaM ma Ni badzre sarba ta thA ga ta a b+hi She ke hU~M/_rig pa'i rgyal po des dkyil 'khor du mngon par dbang bskur bas mngon 
[\[19b.3\]]par dbang bskur ma thag tu/_de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor rigs thun mong ba'i dam tshig rdo rje chen po'i tshul gyis sa gzhi byin gyis brlabs so/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs dang /_pad+ma'i rigs dang /_rdo rje'i rigs dang /_nor bu'i rigs rnam par thar 
[\[19b.4\]]pa chen po'i dkyil 'khor/_bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar cher 'khrigs pa'i dkyil 'khor du brtags pa mthong ngo /_/rang rang gi dge ba'i rtsa bas rig pa'i rgyal po'i dkyil 'khor ba'i lha thams cad mthong ngo /_/rmad du byung ba'i cho 'phrul che zhing che ba dag byung ngo /_/don yod pa'i rnam par 'phrul 
[\[19b.5\]]pa chen po shin tu ngo mtshar ba dag mthong ngo /_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gis dkyil 'khor chen po'i rnam par 'phrul pa 'di lta bu mthong nas don yod pa'i mchod pa'i nor bu rdo rje byin gyis rlobs pa de bzhin gshegs pa'i 
[\[19b.6\]]mngon par dbang bskur ba bcom ldan 'das kyis rjes su gnang ba dang ldan pa'o/_/oM a mo g+ha pU dza ma Ni pad+ma badzre ta thA ga ta bi lo ki te sa man+ta pra sa ra hU~M/_'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gis don yod pa mchod pa'i nor bu'i snying po byin ma thag tu mchod pa rgya chen po rnam pa 
[\[19b.7\]]sna tshogs pa du ma rab tu byung ste/_ji ltar rang rang gi cho ga ji lta ba bzhin du mchod pa chen po rnams rab tu byung ngo /_/lha rdzas rnam pa sna tshogs pa rnam par bkra ba yangs pa rgya chen po go skabs kun tu gnas par gyur to/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa che zhing che ba'i byin gyis brlabs 
[\[20a\]]
[\[20a.1\]]kyi bkod pa rig sngags kyi dkyil 'khor chen po yang dag par mthong ngo /_/de nas rig pa'i rgyal po don yod pa pad+ma dri ma med pa bcom ldan 'das la lan gsum bskor ba byas nas zhabs gnyis la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te/_bcom ldan 'das las ring por 'phags pa spyan 
[\[20a.2\]]ras gzigs dbang phyug las mngon du phyogs nas stan la 'dug go/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis gnod sbyin gyi sde dpon chen po phyag na rdo rje la bka' stsal pa/_phyag na rdo rje 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi rnam par 'phrul ba/_ting nge 'dzin gyi stobs byin gyis rlob pa'i khyad 
[\[20a.3\]]par/_phyag rgya chen po dang /_gsang sngags dang /_gzungs dang /_cho ga zhib mo'i mthu stobs pad+ma yangs pa chen po'i gsang ba don yod zhags pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa rig pa'i rgyal po'i de kho na nyid kyi snying po grub pa dam tshig dang rjes su mthun pa mthu stobs dang yon tan 'byung ba la phan 
[\[20a.4\]]pa/_de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi snying po'i rnam par 'phrul pa yid kyi shing rta re ba thams cad kyi mchog rab tu sbyin pa la ltos/_'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gis re ba thams cad yongs su rdzogs par rjes su sbyin te rig pa'i rgyal po 'di/_phyi ma'i dus phyi ma'i tsha sangs 
[\[20a.5\]]rgyas kyi mdzad pa byed par 'gyur ro/_/rig pa'i rgyal po 'di sems can thams cad kyi snang bar byed par 'gyur ro/_/skyabs dang /_dpung gnyen dang /_'khras dang /_yid kyi shing rta re ba thams cad yongs su rdzogs par byed do/_/sdig pa thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed do/_/dgra thams 
[\[20a.6\]]cad rab tu 'dul bar byed do/_/bgegs dang log par 'dren pa thams cad rnam par 'jig par byed do/_/char ches pa rab tu zhi bar byed do/_/char med na char 'bebs so/_/rlung dang /_sad dang /_ser ba ldog par byed do/_/mu ge'i dgon pa rab tu zhi bar byed do/_/nad thams 
[\[20a.7\]]cad ldog par byed do/_/gdug pa thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed do/_/tshe dang nad med pa 'phel bar byed do/_/bsod nams sbyin pa dang /_dbang phyug sbyin pa dang /_nor sbyin pa dang /_'bru 'phel bar byed do/_/de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'phags pa spyan ras 
[\[20b\]]
[\[20b.1\]]gzigs dbang phyug gis don yod pa dri ma med pa'i chu skyes kyi gzungs kyi snying po'i yo byad dang /_cho ga bklags pas 'grub pa'i dngos grub gsungs so/_/de nas snying po 'di bklags pas 'grub par 'gyur te/_rig pa'i rgyal po bklags pas dkyil 'khor gyi lha thams cad 'gugs par 
[\[20b.2\]]'gyur ro/_/stong phrag brgya bzlas pas pad+mo yangs pa'i dkyil 'khor dang /_gzhal med khang gi rgyan mtha' dag dang /_rig pa'i rgyal po dang bcas pa'i lha rnams dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang /_'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi gzhal med khang dang dkyil 
[\[20b.3\]]'khor dang gzhal med khang mtha' dag mthong ngo /_/de la lha rnams rab tu 'jug par byed do/_/dam tshig shes par yang 'gyur ro/_/rig sngags kyi dkyil 'khor du mngon par dbang bskur bar yang 'gyur ro/_/phyag rgya rim par phye ba dang rjes su mthun par yang 'gyur ro/_/dang po rmi lam 
[\[20b.4\]]mthong ba tsam gyis 'gro'o/_/gnyis par rmi lam mthong ba la de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs kyi dkyil 'khor mthong ngo /_/de bzhin gshegs pa dang byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs thams cad dang /_rig sngags kyi dkyil 'khor gyi lha rnams kyis rang gi rgyan dang dkyil 'khor brtags pa mthong ngo /_/
[\[20b.5\]]gzhal yas khang rgya chen po dang bcas pa yang mthong ngo /_/der yang rab tu 'jug par byed de/_dam tshig kyang 'dzin par byed do/_/phyag rgya dang /_dkyil 'khor dang /_gsang sngags dang /_mngon par dbang bskur ba yang so sor thob bo/_/rig sngags dang bcas pa'i dngos grub rab 
[\[20b.6\]]tu thob bo/_/gsum par rmi lam mthong ba na/_rdo rje rigs kyi dkyil 'khor mthong ste de bzhin gshegs pa dang byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs thams cad dang rig sngags kyi dkyil 'khor gyi lha rnams dang bcas pa rang gi rgyan dang dkyil 'khor du brtags pa dang /_gzhal med khang rgya chen po 
[\[20b.7\]]dang bcas pa mthong ngo /_/de la yang rdo rje 'chang rab tu 'jug par byed do/_/dam tshig gi phyag rgya sbyin par byed do/_/mngon par dbang bskur bas mngon par dbang skur bar byed do/_/rig sngags thams cad dang /_don yod pa'i rim par phye ba'i dngos grub chen po dang /_mngon par 
[\[21a\]]
[\[21a.1\]]dbang bskur bas mngon par dbang skur bar byed do/_/rdo rje'i rigs kyi dngos grub chen po dang /_dam tshig thams cad so sor thob bo/_/bzhi par rmi lam mthong ba na/_nor bu'i rigs chen po'i dkyil 'khor dang phyag rgya'i rim par phye ba dang cho ga zhib mo dang /_rang gi rgyan dang 
[\[21a.2\]]dkyil 'khor mthong ngo /_/de bzhin gshegs pa dang byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs thams cad lha'i tshogs dang dkyil 'khor du gnas pa'i lha dang /_rig pa'i rgyal po yang mthong ste/_de la yang rab tu 'jug par byed do/_/phyag rgya dang /_dam tshig dang /_cho ga dang bcas pa rjes su thob ste/_
[\[21a.3\]]mngon par dbang bskur ba yang rab tu thob bo/_/dngos grub chen po dang mchog sbyin yang so sor thob cing thams cad du mngon par dbang bskur bar yang 'gyur ro/_/lnga par rmi lam mthong ba na rnam par thar pa'i dkyil 'khor du brtags pa dang /_don yod zhags pa'i dkyil 'khor du brtags pa dang /_
[\[21a.4\]]po Ta la ka'i ri'i gzhal med khang dang bcas pa mthong ngo /_/de bzhin gshegs pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs thams cad dang /_lha dang bcas pa dang /_rig pa'i rgyal po'i dkyil 'khor kyi lha rnams mthong ste/_de la yang 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug rang nyid sems 
[\[21a.5\]]can de la rab tu 'jug par byed do/_/dam tshig dang phyag rgya dang /_dkyil 'khor dang gsang sngags dang gzungs kyi dngos grub so sor thob po/_/rang nyid kyang don yod pa'i rig sngags kyi dbang gis mngon par dbang bskur te/_mngon par dbang skur bar byed do/_/cho ga zhib mo thams cad 
[\[21a.6\]]dang /_dngos grub kyang rjes su stsol bar byed do/_/mchog thams cad rab tu sbyin pa so sor thob bo/_/drug par rmi lam mthong ba na/_dkyil 'khor chen po mthong ste/_'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug phyag stong ba mthong ngo /_/gzhal med khang dang bcas pa'i byang chub sems 
[\[21a.7\]]dpa'i tshogs dang /_lha dang /_klu dang /_gnod sbyin dang dri za dang /_lha ma yin dang /_nam mkha' lding dang /_mi'am ci dang /_lto 'phye chen po mthong ste de la yang rab tu 'jug par byed do/_/phyag rgya dang gsang sngags dang dam tshig dang mngon par dbang bskur ba so sor thob ste/_rig sngags 
[\[21b\]]
[\[21b.1\]]thams cad kyi dngos grub kyi mchog sbyin pa so sor thob bo/_/bdun par rmi lam chen po mthong bas de bzhin gshegs pa byang chub kyi snying por gshegs nas rdo rje'i gdan la bzhugs pa mthong ste/_chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba dang /_bdud 'dul ba mthong ngo /_/de la yang de bzhin 
[\[21b.2\]]gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad phyag gser gyi kha dog can brkyang ste/_rang nyid kyi phyag gis bzung nas/_rab tu 'jug par byed do/_/rang nyid mngon par dbang bskur zhing mngon par dbang skur bar byed do/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs thams cad kyis dam tshig dang /_
[\[21b.3\]]phyag rgya dang /_gsang sngags kyi dngos grub kyi mchog sbyin pa rjes su stsol bar byed do/_/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing ston par byed do/_/nad sna tshogs pa dang ldan pa'i 'jig rten pa thams cad rig sngags 'chang gi rgyal po gcig por 'gyur ro/_/skye ba brje ba 
[\[21b.4\]]'chi 'pho ba'i dus su dran pa nye bar gnas shing dus byed par 'gyur ro/_/bde ba can gyi 'jig rten gyi khams su pad+ma las brdzus te skye ba'i skal ba dang bcas par 'gyur ro/_/don yod pa'i rig sngags 'chang gi 'khor los sgyur bar skye bar 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa bye ba 
[\[21b.5\]]khrag khrig brgya stong phrag dgu bcu rtsa dgus mchod par byed do/_/yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas mngon sum du mthong nas nye bar ston par byed do/_/thams cad du yang de rnams rang rang gi sangs rgyas kyi zhing nye bar ston par byed do/_/thams cad du yang /_rang gi gsang ba de bzhin gshegs

[\[21b.7\]]'khor gyi rim par phye ba dang /_gsang sngags dang /_gzungs sngags rnams la mngon du phyogs par 'gyur ro/_/skye ba skye bar yang rang gi bsod nams kyi las kyi tshogs dge ba'i rtsa ba rjes su dran par 'gyur ro/_/thams cad du gnas thams cad yongs su dag pa'i las kyi sgrub thabs kyi cho ga'i 
[\[22a\]]
[\[22a.1\]]dngos grub tu 'gyur ro zhes rtsa ba'i snying po'i dngos grub bla na med pa bklags pas 'grub pa'o/_/de nas 'di'i yo byad dang cho ga sems can thams cad kyi re ba yongs su rdzogs par byed pa'i dngos grub tu 'gyur ro/_/sdig pa thams cad nges par sreg pa la brgya rtsa brgyad bzlas pa'i

[\[22a.3\]]nye bar bsags pa 'di dang /_sdig pa thams cad yongs su zad pa nyid dang /_dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad yongs su rdzogs pa dang /_bdag zhum pa dang 'khrugs pa mtha' dag bzlas pa tsam gyis yang dag par smra bar byed cing mi snang bar byed do/_/rims thams cad la skud pa dkar po la 
[\[22a.4\]]mdud pa nyi shu rtsa gcig bya ste/_brgya rtsa brgyad bzlas pas rims thams cad yongs su grol bar byed do/_/nad thams cad la byabs pas yongs su grol bar byed do/_/mtshon chas sam rma bya'i sgros sam/_mda' yis yang rung ste nad thams cad las yongs su grol bar byed do/_/gdon thams cad 
[\[22a.5\]]la tshon skud sna lnga'i skud pa la mdud pa lan nyi shu rtsa gcig byas te bzlas brjod lan brgya rtsa brgyad byas te gdon gyis zin pa de'i mgul bar bcings te lan bdun du bya ba par bya'o/_/gu gul bdug pa sbyin par bya/_bzlas brjod lan bdun bya bzhin pa dang bcas pas gang dang gang gi 
[\[22a.6\]]gdon gyis zin par gyur pa de yang rang gi lus kyis nges par 'gro ste/_rig sngags 'chang gi mdun du nye bar 'dug nas thams cad smra bar byed do/_/rang gis dris pa de thams cad smra bar byed do/_/bka' bsgo ba thams cad byed par 'gyur ro/_/'thab pa dang /_'thab rtsod dang /_rtsod pa dang /_
[\[22a.7\]]'gyed pa rnams thams cad la smra ba'i mchog tu 'gyur ro/_/gzhan yang bsam gtan dang /_sbyin sreg gis yongs su grol lo/_/khar mur te/_rgyal po'i khab tu zhugs na rgyal po 'khor dang bcas pa dbang du gyur nas gnas so/_/dug 'thungs pas chu la yongs su bzlas te sbyin par 
[\[22b\]]
[\[22b.1\]]bya ste dug med par 'gyur ro/_/dug gis zos pa la byab par bya ste/_dug med par 'gyur ro/_/dug dang byad stems dang /_gsang sngags dang /_phye ma'i sbyor ba thams cad la dri dang bsres pa'i bum pa la stong phrag brgyad yongs su bzlas pa byas la bkru bar bya ste/_bkrus pa tsam 
[\[22b.2\]]gyi mdo la thams cad nyams par byed do/_/gang yang lus zad pa dang nyams pa dang /_bskams pa dang /_rgyal po'i dpal med pa dang /_lus yongs su gdungs pa la rig sngags 'chang des ma reg pas dri'i chus yongs su gang ba'i bum pa dgu yungs kar gyis gtor te stong phrag

[\[22b.5\]]pas khrus byas shing gos dkar po dgus g.yogs par bya'o/_/'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug la mchod pa bya zhing /_dkon mchog gsum la mchod pa bya'o/_/ji ltar 'byor pa'i bram ze'i ston mo yon dang bcas pa bya'o/_/de nas rang nyid byams pa dang ldan par bya'o/_/ji

[\[23a.1\]]par byed pa'i 'jigs pa med par 'gyur ro/_/sdig pa dang sgrib pa dang /_kun nas nyon mongs pa thams cad las yongs su grol bar 'gyur ro/_/ring du 'tsho bar 'gyur ro/_/nad thams cad dang bral bar 'gyur ro/_/skye ba brjes nas bde ba can gyi 'jig rten

[\[23b\]]
[\[23b.1\]]mgon pos 'di gsungs so/_/rgyal po'i 'jigs pas 'jigs pa rnams kyis yungs kar la yongs su bzlas nas rgyal po rnams kyi sgor gtor bar bya ste/_rgyal po'i 'jigs pa'i 'jigs pa thams cad las yongs su grol bar 'gyur te/_thams cad du yang rgyal ba ster bar 'gyur 
[\[23b.2\]]ro/_/na le sham la stong rtsa brgyad yongs su bzlas te/_na le sham gyi 'bras bu kha'i rtse mor bcug ste bcangs na/_gang smras pa de dbang du 'gyur ro/_/gang blangs pa de so sor rnyed do/_/gnas thams cad du 'gro ba rnams la rgyal ba'i mchog chen po sbyin par 'gyur ro/_/klu'i char 
[\[23b.3\]]'bebs pa la bal po se'u'i lcug mas bya ste gang du 'dod pa der/_klu'i char 'bebs so/_/klu'i mtshor song nas mtshan mo bdun du zho dang /_sbrang rtsi dang /_mar dang /_yungs kar gyi sbyin sreg lan stong rtsa brgyad du byas na/_gter chen po rnyed par 'gyur ro/_/dbang phyug thob 
[\[23b.4\]]pa la 'bras yos dang /_yungs kar dang /_mar dang bsres pas sbyin sreg lan stong rtsa brgyad du byas na dbang phyug chen po so sor thob par 'gyur ro/_/dgra 'joms pa la sA lu'i phub ma dang /_lan tshwa dang /_tsha ba'i til mar dang /_bsres pas lan stong rtsa brgyad sbyin sreg byas na 
[\[23b.5\]]dgra thams cad med par 'gyur ro/_/dgra rnams thams cad rab tu 'dul bar 'gyur ro/_/skrod par 'dod pas/_de nyid kyi thal ba las lan nyi shu rtsa gcig tu bzlas te/_dgra'i sgor gtor bar bya ste/_mtshan mo gcig tu mi gnas te/_phyogs dang phyogs mtshams rnams 
[\[23b.6\]]su 'bros par byed do/_/'khrug pa'i g.yul ngor 'jug par 'dod pas yungs kar la lan stong phrag brgyad du yongs su bzlas te/_'khrug pa'i g.yul ngo'i dbus su gtor bar bya ste/_thams cad du rmugs pa dang /_rengs pa dang /_rmongs par 'gyur te thams cad du rgyal ba ster 
[\[23b.7\]]bar 'gyur zhing /_thams cad phyogs dang phyogs mtshams rnams su 'bros par byed do/_/gnas na gnas pa thams cad kyi yang rgyal ba ster bar 'gyur ro/_/thams cad du thogs pa med pa'i stobs dang /_brtson 'grus dang /_pha rol gnon par 'gyur ro/_/ldong ros la lan 
[\[24a\]]
[\[24a.1\]]stong phrag brgyad yongs su bzlas te/_mig gi mig sman du byas na mi snang bar 'gyur te/_'byung po dang /_gnod sbyin dang /_srin po thams cad kyis mi mthong bar 'gyur ro/_/gi waM la lan stong phrag brgyad yongs su bzlas te thig le byas nas bu ga thams cad du rab tu 
[\[24a.2\]]'jug par byed cing /_bu ga'i sgo thams cad bye ste sgor gnas par byed do/_/gang du 'dod pa der 'jug par byed do/_/gi waM gis snying gar thig le byas nas/_gang du 'jug par byed pa de dang de nyid du/_mchod pa chen por 'gyur te/_nor che ba dang /_'bru 'phel ba thob bo/_/lag pa gnyis la 
[\[24a.3\]]byugs pas gang la reg pa de dbang du 'gyur te/_gang blangs pa de thams cad so sor rnyed do/_/rgyal po dbang du byed par 'dod pas/_lag pa g.yas pa la gi waM gis byugs nas lan stong rtsa brgyad yongs su bzlas te/_rgyal po'i sgo la thal lcag sbyin par bya ste/_rgyal po dang btsun 
[\[24a.4\]]mo sras dang 'khor dang bcas pa bdag nyid dang nor gyis kyang dbang du 'gyur ro/_/bud med dbang du byed pa la me tog dang 'bras bu la lan stong phrag brgya rtsa brgyad du yongs su bzlas nas sbyin par bya/_yang na mngag par bya ste/_'phral nyid du dbang du byed par 'gyur ro/_/gang du 'dod pa 
[\[24a.5\]]de dang de nyid du 'gro'o/_/slar yang 'ong ngo /_/yang na ming smos pas nam mkha' la 'phang bar bya ste/_gang gi ming smos pa de 'gugs par 'gyur zhing /_mtshan mo 'ong bar byed do/_/mthu stobs kyis slar yang phyir ldog ste 'gro'o/_/skyes pa dbang du byed pa la/_dzA ti dang sna ma'i me 
[\[24a.6\]]tog la lan bdun yongs su bzlas te sbyin par bya'o/_/yang na mngag par bya ste/_reg pa tsam gyis bdag nyid dang nor gyis kyang dbang du byed par 'gyur ro/_/gang smras pa dang /_gang blangs pa de thams cad byed do/_/'bangs dang ci bsgo ba byed par 'gyur ro/_/gzhon nu ma dbang du 
[\[24a.7\]]byed pa la/_'bras bu la lan brgya rtsa brgyad yongs su bzlas te sbyin par bya ba'am/_mngag par bya ste bzung ba tsam gyis dbang du 'gyur ro/_/grib ma bzhin du rjes su 'brang bar 'gyur zhing ji srid 'tsho bar nye bar gnas par 'gyur ro/_/rje rigs dbang du byed pa la/_ba sha ka'i me 
[\[24b\]]
[\[24b.1\]]tog la lan brgya rtsa brgyad yongs su bzlas te/_sbyin par bya ste dbang du gyur nas nye bar gnas so/_/bram ze dbang du byed pas zho dang 'o ma'i bza' ba la lan brgya rtsa brgyad yongs su bzlas nas sbyin par bya ste/_bram ze dbang du byed pa grub bo/_/rgyal rigs dbang du byed pa 
[\[24b.2\]]la/_bu ram dang 'o ma la lan brgya rtsa brgyad yongs su bzlas nas sbyin par bya ste dbang du 'gyur ro/_/dmangs rigs dbang du byed pa la/_gang dang gang gi bza' ba la lan brgya rtsa brgyad yongs su bzlas te sbyin par bya ste zos pa tsam gyi mod la dbang du 'gyur ro/_/rang gi 'dod pa'i bya ba 
[\[24b.3\]]gzhan rnam pa sna tshogs pa bya ba rnams ni bklags pas grub par 'gyur ro/_/don yod pad+ma'i chu skyes kyi rtsa ba'i snying po'i yo byad dang cho ga'i sgrub thabs so/_/de nas don yod pa'i phyag rgya dbang bskur ba'i gzungs kyi yo byad dang /_cho gar 'gyur te/_gzungs 'di'i rig sngags 'chang gis 
[\[24b.4\]]dran pa tsam dang /_bklags pa dang /_phyag rgya bzung ba tsam gyis de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi yid bzhin gyi nor bu'i gsang ba'i phyag rgya dang /_dbang bskur ba'i phyag rgya dran par 'gyur ro/_/rig pa'i rgyal po'i gzungs stong phrag bzlas pa 'dis dus gsum du gshegs pa'i de bzhin 
[\[24b.5\]]gshegs pa thams cad kyis gsang ba'i phyag rgyas mngon par dbang du bskur bar 'gyur ro/_/rig sngags 'chang des kyang dang por rmi lam gyi nang du mthong ngo /_/dus gsum du gshegs pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das thams cad kyis rnam par snang mdzad yid bzhin nor bu'i phyag rgya'i 
[\[24b.6\]]dbang bskur bas/_mngon par dbang skur bar byed do/_/de yang de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs kyis mngon par dbang bskur ba dang /_dkyil 'khor dang /_phyag rgya la rab tu 'jug par byed do/_/de bzhin gshegs pa rang rang rnams kyang de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsang ba'i dbang bskur ba 
[\[24b.7\]]rjes su sbyin par 'gyur ro/_/legs so zhes bya ba'i sgra brjod par mdzad do/_/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad kyi sgo rnams bye bar gyur pa'i sgor mthong ngo /_/gang du 'dod pa der 'jug go/_/des kyang de bzhin gshegs pa'i gang+gA'i klung gi bye ma bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phrag dgu bcu rtsa dgu 
[\[25a\]]
[\[25a.1\]]dang mnyam pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams rig sngags 'chang de srid 'tsho'i bar tu rtag tu rjes su 'brang bar 'gyur ro/_/rtag tu mthong ba dang ldan par gnas so/_/stong phrag gnyis pa bzlas pas/_gnyis par rmi lam mthong bar blta'o/_/pad+ma'i rigs kyi dkyil 
[\[25a.2\]]'khor gang la yang /_yid bzhin gyi nor bu'i phyag rgya dang /_gzungs dang /_gzhal med khang mtha' dag yongs su rdzogs par mthong ngo /_/der yang dus gsum du gshegs pa'i gzhal med khang thams cad mthong ngo /_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i gzhal med khang mthong ngo /_/de la yang de bzhin 
[\[25a.3\]]gshegs pa pad+ma'i rigs thams cad kyis yid bzhin gyi nor bu'i phyag rgya'i dbang bskur bas mngon par dbang bskur bar 'gyur ro/_/rang rang gi de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsang ba'i phyag rgyas mngon par dbang bskur ba dang /_dkyil 'khor rnams ston par mdzad do/_/rig sngags 'chang

[\[25a.5\]]bzhin gyi nor bu chen po'i phyag rgyas mngon par dbang bskur bas mngon par dbang bskur bar 'gyur zhing /_yongs su 'dzin par yang 'gyur ro/_/stong phrag gsum pa bzlas pas rmi lam chen po gsum pa bstan pa mthong ste/_gzhal med khang mtha' dag tu rab tu gnas pa'i rdo rje'i rigs 
[\[25a.6\]]kyi phyag rgya dang /_mngon par dbang bskur ba rjes su stsol bar mdzad do/_/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dus gsum du gshegs shing rab tu bzhugs pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i yid bzhin gyi nor bu rdo rje byin gyis rlob pa'i mngon par dbang bskur ba dang phyag rgya yang mthong ngo /_/de 
[\[25a.7\]]yang dus gsum du gshegs pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa rnams thams cad rig sngags 'chang la rdo rje'i phyag rgyas mngon par dbang bskur ba dang /_yid bzhin gyi nor bu'i phyag rgyas mngon par dbang bskur bas/_yid bzhin gyi nor bu'i phyag rgyas mngon par dbang skur bar mdzad do/_/
[\[25b\]]
[\[25b.1\]]rang rang rnams kyang rdo rje'i phyag rgyas mngon par dbang bskur ba rjes su stsol bar mdzad do/_/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad rdo rje'i dam tshig la rab tu 'jug par mdzad do/_/rdo rje byin gyis brlabs pa'i mngon par dbang bskur ba legs so legs so zhes bya ba'i sgra rjod par mdzad do/_/

[\[25b.3\]]ba thob bo/_de la yang nor bu'i rigs chen po'i dkyil 'khor gyi phyag rgya'i dbang bskur ba thob po/_/rmi lam ston pa de yang dus gsum pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa nor bu'i phyag rgyas dbang bskur ba'i dkyil 'khor rnams mthong ngo /_/dus gsum pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa de rnams 
[\[25b.4\]]thams cad kyang /_rigs sngags 'chang de la nor bu'i rigs kyi dam tshig gsang ba dang /_phyag rgya dang /_dkyil 'khor dang /_mngon par dbang bskur bas yang mngon par dbang skur bar byed do/_/rang rang rnams kyang de bzhin gshegs pa de rnams kyi nor bu gsang ba'i phyag rgyas 
[\[25b.5\]]dbang bskur ba gtsug gi nor bu byin gyis rlob pa dang /_yid bzhin gyi nor bu'i phyag rgyas mngon par dbang skur bar byed do/_/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi nor bu'i dkyil 'khor rnams nye bar ston par mdzad do/_/yid bzhin gyi nor bu'i dngos grub kyi dam 
[\[25b.6\]]tshig gi dbang bskur ba dang /_yid bzhin gyi nor bu'i phyag rgyas mngon par dbang bskur bar bya'o/_/legs so legs so zhes bya ba'i sgra rjod par byed cing rtag tu rjes su 'brang bar 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa de rnams kyang ji srid sangs rgyas kyi bar du de bzhin gshegs pa thams 
[\[25b.7\]]cad kyi rnam par 'phrul pa byin gyis rlob par yang dag par dgongs par 'gyur ro/_/yid bzhin gyi nor bu'i rigs chen po'i phyag rgyas mngon par dbang bskur bar 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi nor bu'i rigs kyi gsang ba'i snying po'i mchog tu 'gyur ro/_/bzlas pa lnga pas 
[\[26a\]]
[\[26a.1\]]rmi lam chen po'i phyag rgya ston par byed pa ni/_dkyil 'khor chen po mtha' dag rdzogs par phyir mi ldog pa'i gnas kyi mngon par dbang bskur bas mngon par dbang bskur bar mthong ngo /_/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis kyang phyir mi ldog pa chen po'i dkyil 
[\[26a.2\]]'khor chen por mngon par dbang skur bar mdzad do/_/dkyil 'khor thams cad dang /_phyag rgya chen po mngon par dbang bskur bar 'gyur ro/_/bzlas pa drug pas rmi lam yang dag par ston pa ni/_de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad byang chub kyi snying por gshegs te rdo rje'i gdan byin 
[\[26a.3\]]gyis rlob pas byin gyis brlabs te/_mngon par dbang bskur bar 'gyur ro/_/dus gsum pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad bzhugs pa mthong ste seng ge'i gdan la bzhugs pa'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa de rnams kyang rang rang gi rig sngags 'chang phyir mi ldog par mngon par

[\[26a.5\]]rmi lam chen po ston pas/_de bzhin gshegs pa mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas te/_chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba dang /_bdud 'dul ba dang /_dam pa'i chos kyi char 'bebs pa dang /_chos kyi sgron me sbar ba dang /_chos kyi rgyal mtshan sgreng ba dang /_dam pa'i chos kyi dung 
[\[26a.6\]]'bud pa dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad phyag rgya chen pos mngon par dbang bskur ba mthong ngo /_/de bzhin gshegs pa de rnams kyis kyang phyag rgyas mngon par dbang bskur ba dang /_chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba dang /_bdud 'dul ba dang /_phyag rgyas dbang bskur bas mngon 
[\[26a.7\]]par dbang skur bar mdzad do/_/legs so legs so zhes bya ba'i sgra rjod par mdzad do/_/phyag rgya chen po dang dkyil 'khor dang /_kun nas gsang ba dang /_rnam par thar pa'i dkyil 'khor de kho na nyid kyi dngos grub dang mngon par dbang skur bar mdzad do/_/de bzhin du bklags 
[\[26b\]]
[\[26b.1\]]pa dang /_bzlas pa tsam gyis cho ga dang las kyi 'bras bu ston par mdzad do/_/don yod pa'i nor bu rnam par snang mdzad kyi phyag rgya chen po'i gzungs kyi rnam par 'phrul pa'i cho ga 'di yang rnam par spyod par 'gyur ro/_/'di bklags pa dang dran pa tsam gyis de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi

[\[26b.3\]]ston par mdzad do/_/de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad ston par mdzad par 'gyur ro/_mtshan smos nas 'dzin par 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad rnam par snang mdzad kyi phyag rgya 'dzin par 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi gsang 
[\[26b.4\]]ba'i mchog tu 'gyur ro/_/sdig pa dang sgrib pa dang /_gdug pa thams cad dang /_mtshams med pa lnga la sogs pa sngon gyi sdig pa dang /_sgrib pa rnam par 'joms par byed do/_/bklags pa tsam dang /_lan cig bzlas pa tsam gyis de thams cad yongs su zad pa dang /_
[\[26b.5\]]yongs su gtugs par 'gyur ro/_/sems can gyi sdig pa'i chos gang cung zad kun tu spyad pa dang /_mtshams med pa lnga byed pa/_mnar med pa la mchog tu gzhol ba'i sems can de'i mdun du lan gsum du dbyangs gsang bstod pas brjod cing /_dran par bya ste de'i 
[\[26b.6\]]sgra thos pa tsam gyi mod la sdig pa dang /_sgrib pa thams cad dang /_mtshams med par sod ba thams cad dang /_mnar med pa la mchog tu gzhol ba rnams yongs su dag par 'gyur ro/_/sems can de yang sdig pa thams cad las yongs su grol bar 'gyur ro/_/yang na sdig

[\[27a\]]
[\[27a.1\]]su de'i steng du gdab cing der gtor bar bya'o/_/gtor ba tsam gyi mod la sems can de gang dang gang du skyes pa de dang de nyid du sems can de'i lus la byang chub chen po'i yan lag gi chu'i dri'i mchog bsgos par 'gyur ro/_/'phral nyid du dmyal ba dang /_dud 'gro 
[\[27a.2\]]dang /[ {]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]dang /_gshin rje'i 'jig rten dang /_lha ma yin gyi lus las yongs su grol bar 'gyur ro/_/sdig pa dang sgrib pa thams cad yongs su zad par 'gyur ro/_/der yang ngan 'gro rnams las 'phos par 'gyur zhing bde ba can gyi 'jig rten gyi khams su skye bar 
[\[27a.3\]]'gyur ro/_/'di la som nyi dang yid gnyis bskyed par mi bya'o/_/gang gi tshe nad drag pos thebs par gyur pa de'i tshe/_rig sngags 'chang gis rna bar bzlas brjod brgya rtsa brgyad sbyin par bya ste/_de yang nad yun ring po las grol te rang gi gnas su 'gyur ro/_/nad kyi sdug bsngal 
[\[27a.4\]]thams cad las yongs su grol bar 'gyur ro/_/spyi bo'i gtsug tu phyag rgyas btab ste lan nyi shu rtsa gcig brjod par bya ste 'phral nyid du yongs su grol lo/_/gal te nag po chen pos rang gi phyag rgyas btab na de ltar de nyid la bltas pas phyag rgya thams cad 'byed par 'gyur ro/_/
[\[27a.5\]]gal te yun ring du ma grol na gang gis phyag rgyas btab par gyur pa de mig gnyis po dag 'byin par 'gyur ro/_/gdon gyis zin pa thams cad la tshon skud sna lnga'i skud pa la bzlas brjod nyi shu rtsa gcig byas nas mdud pa nyi shu rtsa gcig byas la bcing bar bya ste/_gdon dang brjed 
[\[27a.6\]]byed thams cad las yongs su grol bar 'gyur ro/_/nad thams cad la skud pa dkar pos bcing bar bya ste nad thams cad las yongs su grol lo/_/'thab pa dang /_'thab mo dang /_rtsod pa dang /_'gyed pa rnams thams cad du 'gro ba la lan brgya rtsa brgyad yongs su bzlas te kha 
[\[27a.7\]]ton tu bzung na thams cad du smra ba'i mchog tu 'gyur ro/_/na le sham dang /_rgya spos dang /_ru rta dang /_yung ba gnyis dang /_ro'i mig sman la sngags stong rtsa brgyad du bzlas la phye ma zhib mor byas te/_mig sman dang mnyam par sbyar nas gdon gyis zin pa rnams kyi mig la bskus 
[\[27b\]]
[\[27b.1\]]na/_bskus pa tsam gyi mod la gdon thams cad yongs su grol lo/_/gal te yun ring por ma grol na gdon gyi mig gnyis 'byin par 'gyur ro/_/rims nyin bzhi pa la mig gnyis la bsku bar bya'o/_/gal te yun ring du ma grol na mgo bo tshal pa bdun du 'gas par 'gyur ro/_/

[\[27b.3\]]par 'gyur ro/_/shi ba'i mig la bskus na ldang bar 'gyur te/_de smra bar byed do/_/chu dang yungs kar brdegs na slar yang 'gyel bar byed do/_/mgo bo'i zug rngu dang /_gzhogs phyed na ba rnams kyi mig la bsku ste/_phyag rgya thams cad 'jig par byed do/_/rgyal po khros pa gang gis 
[\[27b.4\]]skye bo gnas nas 'byin pa dang /_de bzhin du mtshon cha mang po 'bab pa dang /_phyir rgol ba dang /_dgra bo nyid la yang 'dis bskus pa'i mig gis bltas na thams cad dga' bar 'gyur ro/_/de rnams thams cad rtag tu dbang du gyur nas gnas te 'di la the tshom mi bya'o/_/don yod pa'i 
[\[27b.5\]]phyag rgya'i dbang bskur ba'i snying po dang gzungs kyi cho ga'i sgrub thabs so/_/de nas don yod pa'i dkyil 'khor chen po pad+ma'i dbang bskur ba'i nor bu'i gzungs kyi snying po'i yo byad du 'gyur te de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dkyil 'khor gyi rigs thun mong bar rjes su 'jug par 
[\[27b.6\]]'gyur ro/_/stong rtsa brgyad bzlas pas mi 'pham pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dkyil 'khor pad+ma chen po'i dbang bskur ba yid bzhin gyi nor bu'i pad+ma bcings pas spyi bor mngon par dbang bskur bar 'gyur ro/_/de nas dus gsum pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa de rnams kyis 
[\[27b.7\]]bzlas pa tsam gyi mod la de'i rmi lam mthong ba nye bar ston par byed do/_/dkyil 'khor chen po yangs pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa yang dag par mthong ba dang cho 'phrul ston par byed do/_/gzhal yas khang dang bcas/_rnam par 'phrul pa dang bcas/_lha'i tshogs dang bcas pa'i rig pa'i 
[\[28a\]]
[\[28a.1\]]rgyal po'i dkyil 'khor pa'i lha rnams nye bar ston par byed do/_/'di lta ste/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs kyi dkyil 'khor dang /_pad+ma'i rigs kyi dkyil 'khor dang /_rdo rje'i rigs kyi dkyil 'khor dang /_nor bu'i rigs kyi dkyil 'khor dang /_don yod 
[\[28a.2\]]zhags pa'i snying po rnam par thar pa'i dkyil 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor chen po dang /_rnam par snang mdzad kyi snying po'i dkyil 'khor chen po de rnams thams cad kyang so so so so'i dkyil 'khor du dbang bskur bas mngon par dbang bskur ro/_/rig pa'i rgyal po'i dkyil 'khor gyi lha 
[\[28a.3\]]rnams mtha' dag nye bar ston par byed de/_ji srid du byang chub kyi snying por gshegs pa dang /_chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba dang /_bdud 'dul ba'i bar gyi dkyil 'khor nye bar ston par byed do/_/dus gsum pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa de rnams kyis kyang dkyil 'khor chen po de rnams 
[\[28a.4\]]kyis so so so so'i dkyil 'khor du mngon par dbang bskur bas mngon par dbang skur bar mdzad do/_/dkyil 'khor gyi mchog thams cad du mngon par dbang bskur bas mngon par dbang skur bar mdzad do/_/de nas rig sngags 'chang de de lta bu'i rmi lam chen po mthong nas/_dga' 
[\[28a.5\]]ba chen po rgyas par 'gyur ro/_/dga' ba dang rab tu mgu ba'i snying des kyang don yod pa pad+ma'i dri ma med pa'i mngon par dbang bskur ba'i ting nge 'dzin so sor thob bo/_/de bzhin gshegs pa de rnams kyis kyang /_rig sngags 'chang de la legs so legs so zhes rjod par mdzad 
[\[28a.6\]]de/_rig sngags 'chang chen po de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dkyil 'khor chen po'i rigs thun mong ba'i dam tshig gis mngon par dbang bskur bas khyod mngon par dbang bskur ro/_/grub pa thob pa de rnams rig sngags 'chang chen po dang dkyil 'khor gyi gsang ba'i rjes su 'jug cing /_
[\[28a.7\]]de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sras kyi thu bor 'gyur ro zhes so sor sad do/_/de nas rig sngags 'chang gis/_bzlas pa tsam dang bklags pa tsam gyis de ma thag par rgya chen po'i yon tan gsal ba de lta bu skye bar 'gyur ro/_/slar yang 'di'i gong nas gong du rnam par 'phrul pa ston 
[\[28b\]]

[\[32b.5\]]rnams kyi sems gnas pa'i mchog go/_/mchog med pa rnams la mchog sbyin pa'o/_/rmi lam ngan pa mthong ba rnams la rmi lam chen po'i mchog ston pa'o/_/bkres pa rnams la kha zas kyi mchog sbyin pa'o/_/skom pa rnams btung ba chen po'i ro la yongs su longs spyod par 
[\[32b.6\]]byed pa'o/_/gcer bu rnams kyis gos dang bgo ba so sor thob cing grub pa'o/_/mdze can rnams kyi mdze dang bral bar byed pa'o/_/sha bkra can rnams kyi sha bkra yongs su sbyong bar byed pa'o/_/rims kyis btab pa rnams kyi rims dang bral bar byed pa'o/_/gdon gyis zin pa 
[\[32b.7\]]rnams kyi gdon dang gshed byed dang /_gdul dka' ba rnams 'dul bar byed pa'o/_/dgra bo phyin ci log pa rnams kyi dgra rnams dang bral bar byed pa'o/_/dbul po rnams la nor sbyin par 'gyur ro/_/bu bza' can rnams kyi bu bza' cher 'phel bar 'gyur ro/_/mun pas long 
[\[33a\]]
[\[33a.1\]]bar byas pa thams cad kyi sgron ma'i shing dang /_nyi ma'i 'od zer stong gis kun tu snang ba'o/_/rgyal pos bcom pa rnams la longs spyod rgya chen po cher byin pa'o/_/'khon pa rnams gcig tu byed pa'o/_/ser sna can rnams kyi ser sna shin tu 'joms pa'o/_/'dod chags 
[\[33a.2\]]la chags pa rnams kyi 'dod chags rab tu 'joms pa'o/_/rmongs pa chung ba ma yin pa rnams kyi rmongs pa dang bral bar 'gyur ro/_/mi dge ba'i lam gyis rab tu nyams pa rnams kyi dge ba'i lam gyi yid chos kyi nang du chud par 'gyur ro/_/gzhan gyi dbang du gyur pa'i sems can rnams kyi 'jigs 
[\[33a.3\]]pa med par 'gyur zhing yid chos kyi nang du chud par 'gyur ro/_/log par ltung ba rnams kyis shes rab dang /_blo dang /_shes bzhin dang /_brkam pa rnams kyi blo bsnyen par 'gyur ro/_/sangs rgyas ma mthong ba rnams de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad mthong bar 'dod par 'gyur ro/_/chos 
[\[33a.4\]]thos pa dang bral ba rnams kyi/_chos chen po'i ro rgya chen po thos par 'gyur ro/_/dge 'dun la bsnyen bkur byed pa dang bral ba rnams kyi lha dang /_nyan thos kyi dag 'dun la bsnyen bkur byed par 'gyur ro/_/dbang po nyams pa rnams kyi/_dbang po yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ro/_/
[\[33a.5\]]yan lag rnam par nyams pa'i sems can rnams kyi so so'i yan lag yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' mthong ba dang bral ba rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs chen po mthong bar 'gyur ro/_/lhas yongs su spangs pa rnams kyi lha'i tshogs thams cad kyis 
[\[33a.6\]]yang dag par 'dzin par 'gyur ro/_/nyon mongs pa dang sgrib pas dkris pa'i sems can rnams kyi nyon mongs pa dang /_sgrib pa thams cad las yongs su grol bar 'gyur ro/_/phyag na rdo rje don yod pa'i mchod pa'i nor bu'i snying po'i gzungs spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gis 
[\[33a.7\]]gsungs pa ni de ltar rdzu 'phrul che ste/_de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi mchod pa'i sprin/_byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi mchod pa'i sprin sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad du rin po che sna bdun gyi mchod pa'i sprin gyi char 'bebs pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi de bzhin gshegs 
[\[33b\]]
[\[33b.1\]]pa thams cad dang mtshungs par dus gsum du gshegs shing bzhugs pa rnams kyi dpag bsam gyi shing chen po'i mchod pa'i sprin gyi char 'bebs par byed pa'i mchod sprin 'byung bar 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rigs kyi dam tshig rig sngags kyi dkyil 'khor 
[\[33b.2\]]gyi de kho na nyid sgrub par byed pa/_rmad du byung ba'i dngos grub yang dag par ston par byed pa'o/_/rig sngags kyi dkyil 'khor gyi lha thams cad dang /_rig pa'i rgyal po'i lha rnams dang /_gsang sngags kyi lha thams cad spyan 'dren zhing 'gugs par byed pa'o/_/'di ni pha rol tu 
[\[33b.3\]]phyin pa drug yongs su rdzogs par byed pa'o/_/'di ni thams cad du gsang ba'i sgo ston par byed pa'o/_/lag na rdo rje don yod pa'i mchod pa'i snying po'i gzungs 'di ni dus ma yin pa'i char dang /_mu ge dang /_dus kyi chu rnam par nyams pa la so sor bskyed pa'o/_/de nas rig sngags 
[\[33b.4\]]'chang gtsang mas khrus byas gos gtsang ba bgos te/_yid rtse gcig tu byas na sems can thams cad la mchog tu brtse ba dang byams pa'i sems bsgom par bya'o/_/mchod rten gyi yan lag dang nye ba'am/_khang stong ngam/_ri bo'am/_nags thibs po dang /_dgon pa dang /_skyed mos tshal lam/_
[\[33b.5\]]dge 'dun gyi kun dga' ra ba'am/_chu klung ngam/_mtshe'u'am/_rdzing bu'am/_klu gnas pa'i mtsho'am/_lha'i gnas sam/_rgyal po'i pho brang yang rung ste/_der rig sngags 'chang gtsang mar gnas pas sa gzhi yongs su brtags pa cha mnyam pa mdzes par khru gnyis kyi tshad kyi dkyil 'khor 
[\[33b.6\]]byas la sa dang ba byung du ldan pas/_gru bzhi par cha gzhal te/_legs par byi dor byas la shin tu 'jam par bya'o/_/der de'i steng du tsan+dan dang /_dri'i chus byug par bya'o/_/gur gum gyis rnam pa sna tshogs pa'i mtshan ma dang phyag rgya rnams ri mor bri'o/_/de la dkyil 'khor gyi dbus kyi snying 
[\[33b.7\]]po/_/klu mgo bo dgu pa skyil mo krung gis 'dug pa bri'o/_/grwa bzhi'i nang du chud par don yod zhags pa bri'o/_/dkyil 'khor gyi phyi rol gyi khor yug tu ji ltar thob pa'i phyag rgya dang /_gsang sngags rnams bri bar bya'o/_/sgo bzhi rnam par dbye bar bya ste/_ji ltar 'byor pa'i gtor ma 
[\[34a\]]
[\[34a.1\]]sna tshogs pa'i ro'i gtor ma dkar po dang /_me tog dang /_bdug pa dang /_dri dang /_mar me dang /_gang ba'i bum pa dang /_gtor ma dang /_bdug pa'i pog phor gyis nye bar mdzes par bya'o/_/rig sngags 'chang gis ches mngon du/_don yod zhags pa lan bdun bzlas bar

[\[34a.3\]]pa'i cho ga bya'o/_/don yod pa'i mchod pa nor bu'i snying po lan stong rtsa brgyad du bzlas brjod bya'o/_/nA ga ge sar dang /_na la di na dang /_rgya spos dang /_gur gum dang /_tsan+dan dang /_yungs kar dang /_li kha ra mnyam par sbyar ba'i bdug pa la bzlas brjod brgya rtsa brgyad bya ste/_klu'i dkyil 'khor du

[\[34b.1\]]pos 'byin par byed do/_/yang dag pa kho nar char gyi rgyun ltung ngo /_/yang na/_klu'i ras ris bya ste/_khru gang gi tshad la kun nas gru bzhi pa'i tshad kyi ras la ri mor bya'o/_/de'i dbus su rig pa'i rgyal po 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug bri bar bya ste/_skyil mo krung gis 'dug pa/_phyag

[\[35a.1\]]smras pa/_rig sngags 'chang gis bka' bsgo ba ci yin pa bdag gis bgyi'o zhes so/_/rig sngags 'chang gis smra bar bya ste/_klu chen po 'dzam bu'i gling kun tu lta bar gyis shig_/khye'u de yang phyogs bzhi kun tu rnam par lta bar byed do/_/ji lta ji ltar mtshan ma mthong ba de lta de ltar

[\[36b.1\]]'jigs pa rab tu zhi bar 'dod pa'i rig sngags 'chang des rig pa'i rgyal po 'ba' zhig ras la bri bar bya ste/_dpal lha mo'i gzugs dang /_kha dog dang /_rtags dang /_dbyibs dang /_rgyan thams cad kyis rnam par brgyan pa/_'od dpag med kyi cod pan 'chang ba'i rgyal po chen

[\[37a\]]
[\[37a.1\]]pa thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed do/_/rmi lam ngan pa dang /_mtshan ma ngan pa/_bkra mi shis pa'i dngos po thams cad rnam par nyams par byed do/_/yul dang /_grong dang /_dgon pa pa dang /_nags tshang tshing dang /_grong khyer dang /_rgyal po'i pho brang rnams thams cad du yang /_
[\[37a.2\]]bde ba byed par 'gyur ro/_/lo legs pa dang /_dga' bar 'os pa dang /_sems can thams cad nye bar 'tshe ba med pa dang /_nad med par 'gyur ro/_/sems can de rnams thams cad du yang /_phan tshun du dga' ba dang /_rab tu mgu ba chen pos phan tshun byams pa'i sems su 'gyur ro/_/don yod pa'i 
[\[37a.3\]]mchod pa'i nor bu'i snying po'i gzungs 'di yid rtse gcig pas tshes bco lnga'i nya la nyin mtshan thog thag tu smyung bar gnas pas gtsang ma gos gtsang ma bgo bar bya'o/_/'byung lnga'i nye reg gis gtsang sbra dang /_khrus dang /_dri'i chu tsam gyis rjes su mthun pa'i byug pa byas te/_skyil mo krung du 'dug 
[\[37a.4\]]nas rig pa'i rgyal po la mngon du phyogs nas bzlas brjod bya'o/_/mtshan thog thag tu mchod pa chen po rgyun mi 'chad par bsgrub par bya'o/_/dri'i til mar gyi me dbul bar bya zhing /_bgrang phreng dpag tu med pa byas pa'i bzlas brjod dbul bar bya'o/_/lan re re bzlas pas lan brgya stong 
[\[37a.5\]]phrag yongs su bzlas par 'gyur ro/_/ji srid du rig pa'i rgyal po'i bris sku 'bar bar byed do/_/'di lta ste/_'od zer sna tshogs pa 'byin par byed do/_/rig sngags 'chang dga' bar byed cing /_spyi bo'i gtsug nas 'bar bar byed do/_/nam mkha' nas rig sngags 'chang khyod kyis bzlas brjod kyi 
[\[37a.6\]]cho ga mchog tu byas pa legs so legs so zhes sgra 'byin par byed do/_/'di ni rig sngags 'chang rnams kyi mchog gi bzlas pa'i cho ga ste/_khyod kyis don yod pa'i zhags pa'i snying po rnam par thar pa'i dkyil 'khor dang /_byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag byas par grub 
[\[37a.7\]]bo/_/khyod kyis don yod pa'i mchod pa'i nor bu'i snying po'i gzungs kyi dngos grub kyang grub bo/_/khyod kyis de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor dang /_phyag rgya rim par phye ba dang /_thun mong gi snying po dang gzungs smras pas/_rig pa 'dzin pa'i mchog de thams 
[\[37b\]]
[\[37b.1\]]cad rjes su mthun par stsol bar 'gyur ro/_/'di lta ste gsang sngags kyi dngos grub bam/_gzungs kyi dngos grub bam/_cho ga zhib mo'i dngos grub bam/_rigs thun mong ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i dngos grub bam/_phyag rgya'i dam tshig gi dngos grub bam/_nam mkha' la 'gro ba'am/_mi 
[\[37b.2\]]snang ba'am/_gzugs sna tshogs su bsgyur ba'am/_mig 'phrul lam/_rdzu 'phrul ston pa'am/_klu'i gzhal med khang ngam/_lha'i gzhal med khang ngam/_gnod sbyin gyi gzhal med khang ngam/_dri za'i gzhal med khang ngam/_lha ma yin gyi gzhal med khang ngam/_nam mkha' lding gi gzhal med khang ngam/_
[\[37b.3\]]mi'am ci'i gzhal med khang ngam lto 'phye chen po'i gzhal med khang ngam/_nyi ma'i gzhal med khang ngam/_zla ba'i gzhal med khang ngam/_skar ma'i tshogs kyi gzhal med khang ngam/_rgyu skar gyi gzhal med khang ngam/_srin po'i gzhal med khang ngam/_'dod pa'i gzugs kyi gzhal med khang yang rung ste de 
[\[37b.4\]]thams cad rjes su 'dzin no/_/'di ni khyod kyis bya ba thams cad bklags pas 'grub cing bzlas pa tsam gyis 'grub par 'gyur ro/_/kye rig sngags 'chang bya ba rnam pa sna tshogs pa de rnams rjes su 'dzin no/_/'di lta ste/_nor sgyur bar smra ba dang /_zhing smra ba dang /_nor bu smra ba dang /_zhags pa'i dngos 
[\[37b.5\]]grub dang /_mig sman sgrub pa dang /_thig le sgrub pa dang /_dbang phyug dang /_blon po dang /_bkrabs pa dang /_mdun na 'don nyid dang /_stobs che ba nyid dang /_longs spyod la dmigs pa dang /_yul gyi bdag po nyid dang /_dbang phyug dang /_nor 'phel ba dang /_gter g.yo ba dang /_nyo tshong gi grogs 
[\[37b.6\]]byed pa dang /_'gugs pa dang /_khung du 'jug pa dang /_ri la 'jug pa dang /_nags su 'jug pa dang /_me la 'jug pa dang /_sa 'og tu 'jug pa dang /_klu'i char 'bebs pa dang /_char gyi rgyun dang /_ser ba'i rgyun dang /_ser ba 'bebs pa dang /_grong lta ba dang /_g.yul du 'jug pa dang /_rig sngags 
[\[37b.7\]]'chang nyid dang /_sangs rgyas kyi zhing mthong ba nyid dang /_ldong ros kyi mig sman sgrub pa dang /_ral gri sgrub pa dang /_rdo rje sgrub pa dang /_rtse gsum sgrub pa dang /_'khor lo sgrub pa dang /_dung sgrub pa dang /_gtun shing sgrub pa dang /_dgra sta sgrub pa dang /_mda' bo che sgrub pa dang /_tho ba sgrub pa dang /_dril bu sgrub pa dang /_rdzas sgrub pa 
[\[38a\]]
[\[38a.1\]]dang /_sman sgrub pa dang /_bcud kyis len sgrub pa dang /_ro langs slong bar byed pa dang /_rang gi gnas 'bebs pa dang /_gos 'bebs pa dang /_rims 'bebs pa dang /_shi ba slong bar byed pa dang /_gdon thams cad 'bebs pa dang /_thod pa 'bebs pa dang /_bum pa 'bebs pa dang /_
[\[38a.2\]]bum bu 'bebs pa dang /_lteng ka 'bebs pa dang /_dril bu 'bebs pa dang /_tho ba 'bebs pa dang /_ral gri 'bebs pa dang /_me long 'bebs pa dang /_pad+ma 'bebs pa dang /_bud med dang /_skyes pa dang /_khye'u dang /_bu mo 'bebs pa dang /_mdzes par byed pa dang /_gar byed pa de rnams thams cad bklags 
[\[38a.3\]]pas grub pa dang bzlas pas 'grub pas rig sngags 'chang rjes su 'dzin te/_don mtha' yas shing don mtha' yas pa de rnams thams cad grub pa'i ched du gyis shig ces so/_/dgos pa thams cad yongs su rdzogs par byed de/_rig sngags 'chang khyod dge ba 'grub cing rig pa ste/_rig sngags 
[\[38a.4\]]'chang grub pa khyod kyis 'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i bya ba byed par 'gyur ro/_/rig sngags 'chang khyod shi 'phos nas bde ba can gyi 'jig rten gyi khams su 'gro'o/_/dus gsum gyi de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis khyod phyir mi ldog par lung bstan no/_/'di ni skye ba tha ma ste/_

[\[38a.6\]]phye ba dang /_cho ga zhib mo la ji srid byang chub la mthar thug pa'i bar du mngon du phyogs par 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin du sgra yis lung ston par byed pa smras nas mi snang bar 'gyur ro/_/de nas rig pa'i rgyal po'i ras ris de'i smin ma'i bar nas 'od zer kha dog sna tshogs pa rnam par bkra zhing bkra ba 'byin par 
[\[38a.7\]]byed de/_de ma thag tu kun nas snang bar byed do/_/kun nas snang bar byas nas kyang /_rig sngags 'chang gi lus la bab ste mi snang bar 'gyur ro/_/mi snang bar gyur pa'i de ma thag tu rig sngags 'chang des ting nge 'dzin stong phrag brgya thob par 'gyur ro/_/'di lta ste phyir mi ldog pa'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_
[\[38b\]]
[\[38b.1\]]rnam par snang bar byed pa'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_kun nas sgo'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_kun nas 'od kyi dkyil 'khor gyi ting nge 'dzin dang /_'od yangs pa'i dkyil 'khor gyi ting nge 'dzin dang /_don yod pa rnam par 'phrul pa'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_don yod pa mchod pa'i nor bu'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_don yod pa'i 
[\[38b.2\]]nor bu zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_dngos grub gsal ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_kun tu gzigs pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_phyag rgya rim par phye ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_dkyil 'khor gsang ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_don yod pa'i gzungs kyi ting nge 'dzin dang /_kun tu gzigs pa'i nor 
[\[38b.3\]]bu'i rgyal po'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_dus kyi rjes su 'brang ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_pad+ma mchog ces bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_pad+ma rnam par blta ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_zhags pa rab tu gnas pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_rtag par khong du chud pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_rgyal 
[\[38b.4\]]ba'i 'od zer zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_rgyu skar kun tu gzigs pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_dngos grub kyi 'od zer dri ma med pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_kun nas nor bu'i gzi brjid ces bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_dbang phyug gi mthu stobs dri ma med pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin 
[\[38b.5\]]dang /_'od zer kun nas snang ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_blo yang dag par 'dzin pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_gsal bar khong du chud pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_phyag rgya kun tu gzigs pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_phyag rgya kun tu gzigs pa'i pad+ma zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin 
[\[38b.6\]]dang /_rtag par char 'bebs pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_dri ma med pa'i snying po zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_dngos grub yangs pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_me tog ldan pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_kun nas kun tu gzigs pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_kun nas kun tu rnam par 
[\[38b.7\]]sbyong ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_lce'i mchog dang ldan pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_yi ge rnam par dag pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_chu yongs su sbyong ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_gzi brjid dang ldan pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_yangs par byin gyis brlabs pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge

[\[39a.1\]]'dzin dang /_blo 'gugs par byed pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_me tog mngon par bkram pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_rnam par 'phrul pa bkod pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_gzungs rnam par dag cing dri ma med pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_gzi brjid yan lag ces bya 
[\[39a.2\]]ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_thams cad yongs su dag pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin so sor thob bo/_/mdor bsdu na ting nge 'dzin 'di rnams bklags pa tsam dang bzlas pa tsam gyis 'jig rten pa'i sgrub pa'i dngos grub so sor thob bo/_/gang zhig bzlas pa tsam gyis 'jig rten pa'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_gsang

[\[39a.4\]]rten las 'das pa'i dngos grub de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi gsang ba rim par phye ba dang /_dkyil 'khor dang /_gsang sngags dang /_phyag rgya de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rigs thun mong bar gyur pa mthong ba'i dngos grub so sor thob bo/_/ting nge 'dzin gyi rnam par 'phrul pa gang gis de 
[\[39a.5\]]bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis dus gsum pa'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_dkyil 'khor gyi gsang ba dang /_gsang sngags dang /_phyag rgya dang /_spyi gtsug kun tu gzigs par 'gyur ro/_/phyag rgya dang /_gsang sngags dang /_dkyil 'khor dang /_cho ga zhib mo gang yin pa de rnams de bzhin 
[\[39a.6\]]gshegs pa thams cad kyi rigs kyi gsang ba'i rgyal mtshan gyi mchog tu kun tu gnas so/_/gang gis skye ba brjes pa'i mod dang /_bsgoms pa'i mod la rgyal mtshan gyi mchog dang ldan pa'i sgo mchog tu gnas so/_/'di lta ste/_don yod pa'i ye shes bkod pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_dpa' 
[\[39a.7\]]bar 'gro ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_rgyal mtshan rtse mo'i dpung rgyan zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_gzungs kyi bdag po zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_dge ba'i me tog gtsang ma zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_spyi gtsug kun tu rnam par blta ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_seng ge rnam par rtse

[\[39b.1\]]ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_'od 'bar bar byed pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_gzi brjid ldan pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_rin chen 'byung gnas zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_gzungs kyi phyag rgya zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_rnam par snang mdzad kyi snying po zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin

[\[39b.4\]]'das pa brgya stong phrag bklags pa dang /_bzlas pa tsam gyis grub par 'gyur ro/_/rig pa'i rgyal po'i gzungs kyi snying po 'di yon tan che ba dang /_phan yon che ba dang /_rdzu 'phrul dang /_rnam par 'phrul pa che ba ste/_don yod pa lcags kyu'i cho ga zhib mo la/_don yod pa nor bu'i mchod pa

[\[40b.1\]]skyes pa'am/_khye'u'am bu mo yang rung ste/_lan cig bzlas pa dang /_dran pa tsam dang bklags pa tsam gyis bsod nams kyi phung po dang /_dge ba'i rtsa ba dang ldan par 'gyur ro/_/bcom ldan 'das sdig pa thams cad zad par gyur pa de ci lags/_bcom ldan 'das 'jig rten pa rnams grub par 
[\[40b.2\]]'gyur bci lags/_'jig rten las 'das pa'i lam yongs su dag par 'gyur ba ci lags/_bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par gzigs par 'gyur ba ci lags/_bcom ldan 'das 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi rtag tu mchog stsol ba de ci lags/_bcom ldan 
[\[40b.3\]]'das bdag la de'i skabs dbye bar mdzad du gsol/_de lta bu'i phan yon phul du byung ba ni bcom ldan 'das mchog tu ngo mtshar bar gyur pa'o/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_lag na rdo rje legs so legs so/_/lag na rdo rje mchog tu rnyed par dka' ba'i dri ba dris pa de ngas ji lta 
[\[40b.4\]]ba bzhin du da lta bshad par bya yi nyon cig_/lag na rdo rje 'di ni bklags pa tsam gyi mod dang /_brjod pa tsam gyi mod la byang chub sems dpa' 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug rtag tu dga' ba dang bcas pas phyag g.yas pa spyi bor 'jog par byed do/_/mngon sum du rang gi

[\[41a\]]
[\[41a.1\]]pa'i sprin gyi rnam par 'phrul pa'o/_/'di ni rdo rje'i snying pa chen po mthong ba'o/_/'di ni mgo'i snying po chen po'o/_/'di ni sna tshogs pa'i gzugs kyi rang bzhin mthong ba'o/_/'di ni don yod pa'i gzugs dang don yod pa'i dngos grub kyi rnam par 'phrul pa'o/_/'di ni des

[\[58b.6\]]a mo g+ha bi lo ki te/_ba ra ba ra/_dr-i D+ha bIr+ya ma hA ba la/_ba la ba la/_ma hA bo d+h+yaM ba/_/ba la bo d+ha ni hU~M phaT swA hA/_gzungs 'di dus rtag tu dran pa'am/_yongs su dag cing spro ba'i yid kyis dran pa tsam gyi mod la brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su dag par 'gyur ro/_/gang 'di yid la byed 
[\[58b.7\]]pa dang ldan pas dge ba sna 'ga' tsam byas pa yang dpag tu med pa byas par 'gyur/_stobs dang ldan par 'gyur/_myur bar 'gyur ro/_/byang chub kyi snying po la 'dug gi bar du brtson 'grus nyams par mi 'gyur/_brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang nam yang mi 'bral bar 'gyur/_brtson 
[\[59a\]]
[\[59a.1\]]pa chen por 'gyur ro/_/dus gsum gyi de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo/_/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po snying rje chen po dang ldan pa 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug la phyag 'tshal lo/_/oM sar+b+ba ta thA ga ta ma hA a mo g+ha kA ru Na/_d+h+yA na/_
[\[59a.2\]]sa mA d+hi/_sar+b+ba bi mo k+Sha/_a pra kam pya ka_/tsu ru tsu ru hU~M phaT swA hA/_gzungs 'di 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi drung du yid la byed pas rtse gcig tu dran par byas na dran pa tsam gyi mod la bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ro/_/gzhan yang ting nge 'dzin stong phrag brgya 
[\[59a.3\]]so sor rab tu thob par 'gyur ro/_/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad du zhugs par 'gyur ro/_/bzlas brjod byas pa tsam gyi mod la ting nge 'dzin thams cad la dbang byed par 'gyur ro/_/dus gsum gyi de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo/_/byang chub sems dpa' sems 
[\[59a.4\]]dpa' chen po snying rje chen po dang ldan pa 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug la phyag 'tshal lo/_/oM a mo g+ha ma hA pradz+nyA pA ra mi tA a ba b+hA sa swa b+hA ba/_sa maM ta s+phar Na/_bud d+hi/_pra sa ra pra sa ra/_sa man ta bud d+hi/_a ba lo ki te/_b+ha ga bA na pradz+nyA a ba lo ki te tsak+Shu ShaM ma hA pradz+nyA par 
[\[59a.5\]]ma ni ma hA pradz+nyA/_pad+ma d+hA ri Ni b+hu dze hU~M phaT swA hA/_gzungs 'di dran pa tsam gyi mod la shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ro/_/thabs dang shes rab sna tshogs kyi dge ba'i blo chen po rab tu 'phel bar 'gyur ro/_/rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad par pha rol tu phyin pa bcu'i blo 
[\[59a.6\]]dang ldan par 'gyur ro/_/phyogs bcu'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang /_'das pa dang /_ma 'ongs pa na 'byung bar 'gyur ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad yid la byed cing /_sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i shes rab kyi blo dang rab tu ldan par 'gyur ro/_/lan 'ga' yang pha 
[\[59a.7\]]rol tu phyin pa drug dang 'bral bar mi 'gyur ro/_/sbyin pa dang /_tshul khrims dang /_bzod pa dang /_brtson 'grus dang /_bsam gtan dang /_shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa las yongs su nyams par mi 'gyur ro/_/byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas kyi bar du nam yang 
[\[59b\]]
[\[59b.1\]]rmongs par mi 'gyur ro/_/chos thams cad kyi de bzhin nyid la yang dag par rjes su song ba zhes bya ba'i de kho na nyid dang rab tu ldan par 'gyur ro/_/blo gros dpag tu med pa dang ldan pa phun sum tshogs par 'gyur ro/_/phyogs bcu'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis rab tu dgongs 
[\[59b.2\]]par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' thams cad rjes su sems par 'gyur ro/_/'phags pa don yod pa'i zhags pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa drug yongs su rdzogs par byed pa'i gzungs chen po rdzogs so//_//paN+Di ta many+dzu shrI warma dang /_lots+tsha ba dge slong blo ldan shes rab kyis
